def _required_environment(name: str) -> str:
    value = os.getenv(name, "").strip()

    if not value:
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"{name} is required for authentication email composition."
        )

    return value


def _create_announcement_components():
    telegram_client = TelegramAnnouncementClient()

    announcement_publisher = TelegramAnnouncementPublisher(
        telegram_client=telegram_client,
    )

    return (
        telegram_client,
        announcement_publisher,
    )


@asynccontextmanager
async def lifespan(
    application: FastAPI,
):
    scheduler_runtime = application.state.scheduler_runtime

    try:
        await scheduler_runtime.start()
    except Exception as exc:
        print(
            "Cybersecurity scheduler startup failed:",
            exc,
        )

    yield

    try:
        await scheduler_runtime.stop()
    except Exception as exc:
        print(
            "Cybersecurity scheduler shutdown failed:",
            exc,
        )

    finally:
        telegram_client = getattr(
            application.state,
            "announcement_telegram_client",
            None,
        )

        if telegram_client is not None:
            try:
                await telegram_client.close()
            except Exception as exc:
                print(
                    "Telegram announcement client shutdown failed:",
                    exc,
                )

        auth_engine = getattr(
            application.state,
            "auth_engine",
            None,
        )

        if auth_engine is not None:
            try:
                await auth_engine.dispose()
            except Exception as exc:
                print(
                    "PostgreSQL authentication engine shutdown failed:",
                    exc,
                )

        provider_usage_engine = getattr(
            application.state,
            "provider_usage_engine",
            None,
        )

        if provider_usage_engine is not None:
            try:
                provider_usage_engine.dispose()
            except Exception as exc:
                print(
                    "PostgreSQL provider-usage engine shutdown failed:",
                    exc,
                )


def create_application() -> FastAPI:
    academy_config = (
        AcademyActivationConfig.from_environment()
    )

    auth_engine = create_async_database_engine()

    auth_session_factory = (
        create_async_session_factory(
            auth_engine,
        )
    )

    auth_repository = (
        PostgreSQLAuthenticationRepository(
            auth_session_factory,
        )
    )

    email_provider_manager = EmailProviderManager()

    authentication_email_sender = AuthenticationEmailSenderAdapter(
        provider_manager=email_provider_manager,
    )

    authentication_email_service = AuthenticationEmailService(
        authentication_email_sender,
        frontend_base_url=_required_environment(
            "EASYCHATBOT_PORTAL_URL",
        ),
    )

    authentication_service = AuthenticationService(
        auth_repository,
        email_service=authentication_email_service,
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Developer Administration
    #
    # Production path:
    #
    #   DeveloperAdminService
    #          |
    #          +--> DeveloperAdminUnitOfWorkFactory
    #                  |
    #                  +--> fresh PostgreSQLDeveloperAdminUnitOfWork
    #                          |
    #                          +--> fresh AsyncSession
    #                          |
    #                          +--> fresh transaction
    #                          |
    #                          +--> auth_user_roles
    #                          |
    #                          +--> developer_admin_audit_events
    #          |
    #          +--> DeveloperAdminAuditService
    #
    # Each Developer Admin mutation receives a fresh Unit-of-Work
    # instance. The role mutation and its security audit evidence
    # therefore remain within the same database transaction while
    # mutable UoW/session state is never shared between operations.
    #
    # The standalone audit repository owns transactions only when its
    # standalone ``record`` operation is used. The production Developer
    # Admin mutation uses ``record_in_session`` through the UoW.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    developer_admin_unit_of_work_factory = (
        PostgreSQLDeveloperAdminUnitOfWorkFactory(
            auth_session_factory,
        )
    )

    developer_admin_audit_service = (
        DeveloperAdminAuditService()
    )

    developer_admin_service = DeveloperAdminService(
        repository=auth_repository,
        unit_of_work_factory=developer_admin_unit_of_work_factory,
        audit_service=developer_admin_audit_service,
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # W15.2.1 Transaction Authorization
    #
    # Transaction authorization is composed once at the application root.
    # The registry and consumer are application-lifetime components and
    # provide their own synchronization boundaries.
    #
    # Authentication assurance remains a separate trusted-evidence
    # boundary. This composition does not manufacture MFA/A2 evidence.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    authorization_service = AuthorizationService()

    transaction_authorization_registry = (
        TransactionAuthorizationRegistry()
    )

    transaction_authorization_consumer = (
        TransactionAuthorizationConsumer(
            transaction_authorization_registry,
        )
    )

    transaction_authorization_service = (
        TransactionAuthorizationApplicationService(
            authorization_service=authorization_service,
            registry=transaction_authorization_registry,
            consumer=transaction_authorization_consumer,
        )
    )

    trusted_clock = SystemTrustedClock()

    developer_admin_transaction_service = (
        DeveloperAdminTransactionService(
            developer_admin_service=developer_admin_service,
            transaction_authorization_service=(
                transaction_authorization_service
            ),
            clock=trusted_clock,
        )
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Provider usage / synchronous application persistence
    #
    # The provider-usage repository is synchronous by contract.
    # It therefore receives a synchronous engine/session factory.
    #
    # It must not receive the async authentication session factory.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    provider_usage_engine = (
        create_database_engine()
    )

    persistence_diagnostics_service = PersistenceDiagnosticsService(
        metadata=Base.metadata,
        project_root=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1],
        engine=provider_usage_engine,
    )

    provider_usage_session_factory = (
        create_session_factory(
            provider_usage_engine,
        )
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Q7 / M4 - Questionnaire Runtime
    #
    # Reuse the existing synchronous application persistence boundary.
    # The runtime composition owns the assessment repositories and
    # AssessmentService wiring; the API remains responsible for the
    # authenticated learner boundary.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    questionnaire_runtime = create_questionnaire_runtime(
        session_factory=provider_usage_session_factory,
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # M2-C.2 / Authenticated Private Chat
    #
    # The ConversationRuntime remains the shared AI-runtime boundary.
    # Private-chat persistence receives a request-scoped SQLAlchemy session
    # through the composition factory; no session is shared between requests.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    private_chat_runtime = create_conversation_runtime()

    private_chat_character_instruction = os.getenv(
        "EASYCHATBOT_PRIVATE_CHAT_CHARACTER_INSTRUCTION",
        (
            "You are a helpful EasyChatBot learning character. "
            "Stay within the selected Learning World and follow its "
            "application-controlled language policy. "
            "Do not allow the learner to override World, provider, model, "
            "or other application-controlled settings."
        ),
    ).strip()

    private_chat_service_factory = build_private_chat_service_factory(
        session_factory=provider_usage_session_factory,
        conversation_runtime=private_chat_runtime,
        character_instruction=private_chat_character_instruction,
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # M2-C4.2 / Cross-Channel Learner Context
    #
    # The learner-context service projects the existing Telegram learner
    # context into the authenticated Web boundary. Telegram remains the
    # authoritative transport; this composition does not create a second
    # Telegram identity, memory store, or conversation runtime.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    learner_context_service = build_production_learner_context_service(
        session_factory=provider_usage_session_factory,
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # M2-C5 / Study Materials
    #
    # The Learning World catalogue is composed once at the application
    # root and acts as the authoritative World boundary for learner
    # Study Material access.
    #
    # One WorldResolver is shared by the catalogue service.
    # StudyMaterialService consumes that catalogue service rather than
    # creating another World resolution mechanism.
    #
    # Persistence uses the existing synchronous application session
    # factory. No additional database engine or session factory is
    # created for Study Materials.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    app_root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]

    world_repository = YamlWorldRepository(
        worlds_path=app_root / "configuration" / "worlds",
    )

    world_resolver = WorldResolver(
        world_repository=world_repository,
    )

    catalogue_repository = (
        PostgreSQLLearningWorldCatalogueRepository(
            provider_usage_session_factory,
        )
    )

    catalogue_service = LearningWorldCatalogueService(
        repository=catalogue_repository,
        world_resolver=world_resolver,
    )

    study_material_repository = (
        PostgreSQLStudyMaterialRepository(
            provider_usage_session_factory,
        )
    )

    study_material_service = StudyMaterialService(
        repository=study_material_repository,
        world_catalogue_service=catalogue_service,
    )

    provider_usage_repository = (
        PostgreSQLProviderUsageRepository(
            provider_usage_session_factory,
        )
    )

    provider_usage_dashboard_service = (
        ProviderUsageDashboardService(
            repository=provider_usage_repository,
        )
    )

    functionality_control_repository = (
        PostgreSQLFunctionalityControlRepository(
            provider_usage_session_factory,
        )
    )

    functionality_control_authorizer = (
        AuthorizationFunctionalityControlAuthorizer()
    )

    functionality_control_service = (
        FunctionalityOperationalControlService(
            authorizer=functionality_control_authorizer,
            repository=functionality_control_repository,
        )
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Project Control / operational health composition
    #
    # Project Control is read-only. It consumes application-owned health
    # and operational services and owns only its own PCD registry and
    # snapshot repository.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    production_health_configuration = (
        ProductionHealthConfiguration(
            database_engine=provider_usage_engine,
            telegram_bot_factory=None,
            llm_provider=None,
            llm_request_get=None,
        )
    )

    production_health_service = (
        ProductionHealthService(
            configuration=production_health_configuration,
        )
    )

    production_readiness_service = (
        production_health_service._readiness_service
    )

    operational_health_snapshot_service = (
        OperationalHealthSnapshotService(
            readiness_service=production_readiness_service,
        )
    )

    operational_event_service = (
        OperationalEventService()
    )

    operational_dashboard_service = (
        OperationalDashboardService(
            health_snapshot_service=operational_health_snapshot_service,
            event_service=operational_event_service,
        )
    )

    privacy_governance_composition = (
        get_privacy_governance_composition()
    )

    project_registry = PostgreSQLProjectRegistry(
        provider_usage_session_factory,
    )

    project_registry_service = ProjectRegistryService(
        project_registry,
    )

    project_discovery_repository = PostgreSQLProjectDiscoveryRepository(
        provider_usage_session_factory,
    )

    project_discovery_service = ProjectDiscoveryService(
        discovery_repository=project_discovery_repository,
        project_registry=project_registry_service,
    )

    project_snapshot_repository = (
        InMemorySnapshotRepository()
    )

    project_control_service = (
        create_project_control_service(
            registry=project_registry,
            snapshots=project_snapshot_repository,
            health_service=production_health_service,
            dashboard_service=operational_dashboard_service,
            case_registry=(
                privacy_governance_composition.case_registry
            ),
        )
    )

    academy_one_week_workflow = None
    announcement_telegram_client = None

    if academy_config.activation_allowed:
        academy_session_factory = (
            auth_session_factory
        )

        (
            announcement_telegram_client,
            _announcement_publisher,
        ) = _create_announcement_components()

        academy_one_week_workflow = (
            create_academy_one_week_workflow(
                session_factory=academy_session_factory,
                telegram_bot=announcement_telegram_client,
            )
        )

    scheduler_runtime = (
        create_cybersecurity_scheduler_runtime(
            academy_one_week_workflow=academy_one_week_workflow,
        )
    )

    application = FastAPI(
        title="EasyChatBot API",
        description="AI character learning platform API",
        version="0.1.0",
        lifespan=lifespan,
    )

    application.state.scheduler_runtime = (
        scheduler_runtime
    )

    application.state.auth_repository = (
        auth_repository
    )

    application.state.authentication_service = (
        authentication_service
    )

    application.state.developer_admin_service = (
        developer_admin_service
    )

    application.state.developer_admin_transaction_service = (
        developer_admin_transaction_service
    )

    application.state.auth_engine = (
        auth_engine
    )

    application.state.provider_usage_engine = (
        provider_usage_engine
    )

    application.state.provider_usage_dashboard_service = (
        provider_usage_dashboard_service
    )

    application.state.functionality_control_service = (
        functionality_control_service
    )

    application.state.private_chat_service_factory = (
        private_chat_service_factory
    )

    application.state.learner_context_service = (
        learner_context_service
    )

    application.state.study_material_service = (
        study_material_service
    )

    application.state.private_chat_character_instruction = (
        private_chat_character_instruction
    )

    application.state.project_control_service = (
        project_control_service
    )

    application.state.project_discovery_service = (
        project_discovery_service
    )

    application.state.production_health_service = (
        production_health_service
    )

    application.state.operational_dashboard_service = (
        operational_dashboard_service
    )

    application.state.announcement_telegram_client = (
        announcement_telegram_client
    )

    application.add_middleware(
        CORSMiddleware,
        allow_origins=[
            "http://localhost:3000",
            "http://127.0.0.1:3000",
            "http://localhost:5173",
            "http://127.0.0.1:5173",
        ],
        allow_credentials=True,
        allow_methods=["*"],
        allow_headers=["*"],
    )

    application.include_router(
        authentication_router,
    )

    application.include_router(
        private_chat_router,
        prefix="/api",
    )

    application.include_router(
        learner_context_router,
        prefix="/api",
    )

    application.include_router(
        study_material_router,
    )

    application.include_router(
        developer_admin_router,
    )

    application.include_router(
        voice_calls_router,
    )

    application.include_router(
        voice_reconnect_router,
    )

    application.include_router(
        voice_call_trigger_router,
    )

    application.include_router(
        voice_notification_acceptance_router,
    )

    operations_center_composition = (
        create_operations_center_composition(
            provider_usage_dashboard_service=(
                provider_usage_dashboard_service
            ),
            functionality_control_service=(
                functionality_control_service
            ),
        )
    )

    application.include_router(
        create_operations_center_router(
            composition=operations_center_composition,
        ),
    )

    application.include_router(
        create_persistence_operations_router(
            persistence_diagnostics_service,
            operator_dependency=require_permission(
                Permission.INTERNAL_DATA_READ,
            ),
        )
    )

    application.include_router(
        create_project_control_router(
            project_control=project_control_service,
        ),
    )

    application.include_router(
        create_project_registry_router(
            project_registry=project_registry_service,
        ),
    )

    application.include_router(
        create_project_discovery_router(
            project_discovery=project_discovery_service,
        ),
    )

    application.include_router(
        create_email_workflows_router(
            session_factory=provider_usage_session_factory,
            operator_dependency=require_permission(
                Permission.INTERNAL_DATA_READ,
            ),
        ),
    )

    application.include_router(
        privacy_governance_router,
    )

    processing_registry = (
        create_beta1_processing_registry()
    )

    screening_service = ScreeningService()

    screening_adapter = (
        ProcessingActivityScreeningAdapter(
            screening_service,
        )
    )

    application.include_router(
        create_privacy_screening_router(
            processing_registry=processing_registry,
            screening_adapter=screening_adapter,
        ),
    )

    application.include_router(
        provider_management_controls_router,
    )

    application.include_router(
        governance_audit_router,
    )

    application.include_router(
        governance_operations_router,
    )

    application.include_router(
        governance_evidence_router,
    )

    application.include_router(
        governance_change_lifecycle_router,
    )

    application.include_router(
        cybersecurity_intelligence_router,
    )

    application.include_router(
        cybersecurity_decision_support_router,
    )

    application.include_router(
        cybersecurity_reports_router,
    )

    application.include_router(
        cybersecurity_live_reports_router,
    )

    # Q7 / M4 - Questionnaire Runtime
    #
    # Authenticated learner runtime endpoint:
    # POST /api/academy/questionnaires/answers
    #
    # Learner identity is derived server-side from the authenticated
    # principal. The runtime reuses the existing AssessmentService and
    # synchronous PostgreSQL persistence boundary.
    application.include_router(
        create_questionnaire_runtime_router(
            runtime=questionnaire_runtime,
        ),
    )

    # Academy World - Static Questionnaires
    #
    # This exposes:
    # GET /api/academy/worlds/{world_id}/questionnaires
    #
    # Questionnaire content remains controlled/static.
    # Recommendation metadata is handled separately.

    application.include_router(
        academy_questionnaires_router,
    )

    return application


app = create_application()


__all__ = [
    "app",
    "create_application",
]

"""Operations Center HTTP routes for Platform Intelligence human review."""
from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, status

from app.api.dependencies.authorization import require_permission
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_review_api import (
    PlatformIntelligenceReviewAPI,
    PlatformIntelligenceReviewNotConfigured,
)
from app.domain.authorization.permissions import Permission


def create_platform_intelligence_review_router(
    *,
    api: PlatformIntelligenceReviewAPI,
) -> APIRouter:
    """Create the read-only PI review router.

    The caller owns composition and dependency injection. No global runtime or
    repository is constructed here.
    """
    if api is None:
        raise ValueError("api is required.")

    router = APIRouter(
        prefix="/api/operations-center/platform-intelligence",
        tags=["operations-center", "platform-intelligence"],
    )
    read_dependency = Depends(require_permission(Permission.INTERNAL_DATA_READ))

    @router.get("/reviews", dependencies=[read_dependency])
    def get_reviews() -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            return api.list_reviews()
        except PlatformIntelligenceReviewNotConfigured as exc:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
                detail=str(exc),
            ) from None

    @router.get("/reviews/{review_item_id}", dependencies=[read_dependency])
    def get_review(review_item_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            result = api.get_review(review_item_id)
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
                detail=str(exc),
            ) from None
        except PlatformIntelligenceReviewNotConfigured as exc:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
                detail=str(exc),
            ) from None

        if result is None:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
                detail="Platform Intelligence review item not found.",
            )
        return result

    @router.get("/escalations", dependencies=[read_dependency])
    def get_escalations() -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            return api.list_escalations()
        except PlatformIntelligenceReviewNotConfigured as exc:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
                detail=str(exc),
            ) from None

    return router


__all__ = ["create_platform_intelligence_review_router"]

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, status
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field

from app.api.dependencies.authorization import require_permission
from app.application.operations_center.dashboard_composition import (
    OperationsCenterComposition,
    create_operations_center_composition,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_review_api import (
    PlatformIntelligenceReviewNotConfigured,
)
from app.application.provider_management.environment import (
    OperationsEnvironment,
)
from app.application.provider_management.functionality_operational_control_service import (
    FunctionalityOperationalControlService,
)
from app.domain.authorization.permissions import Permission
from app.domain.identity.principal import Principal


class FunctionalityStopRequest(BaseModel):
    environment: OperationsEnvironment
    reason: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=2000)


class FunctionalityResumeRequest(BaseModel):
    environment: OperationsEnvironment


def _control_unavailable() -> HTTPException:
    return HTTPException(
        status_code=status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
        detail="Functionality operational control is not configured.",
    )


def _result_as_dict(result: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "functionality_id": result.functionality_id,
        "environment": result.environment,
        "availability": result.availability,
        "action": result.action,
        "reason": result.reason,
        "changed_at": result.changed_at.isoformat(),
        "changed_by": result.changed_by,
    }


def create_operations_center_router(
    *,
    composition: OperationsCenterComposition | None = None,
) -> APIRouter:
    resolved_composition = composition or create_operations_center_composition()

    dashboard_api = resolved_composition.dashboard_api
    chat_operations_api = resolved_composition.chat_operations_api
    functionality_health_service = resolved_composition.functionality_health_service
    functionality_control_service = (
        resolved_composition.functionality_control_service
    )
    event_service = resolved_composition.event_service
    provider_usage_dashboard_service = (
        resolved_composition.provider_usage_dashboard_service
    )
    platform_intelligence_review_api = (
        resolved_composition.platform_intelligence_review_api
    )

    router = APIRouter(
        prefix="/api/operations-center",
        tags=["operations-center"],
    )

    @router.get("/dashboard")
    async def get_dashboard() -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await dashboard_api.get_dashboard()

    @router.get("/chat-operations")
    async def get_chat_operations_dashboard() -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await chat_operations_api.get_dashboard()

    @router.get("/chat-operations/private-classroom")
    async def get_private_classroom_operations() -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await chat_operations_api.get_private_classroom_details()

    @router.get("/chat-operations/announcement-group")
    async def get_announcement_group_operations() -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await chat_operations_api.get_announcement_group_details()

    @router.get("/functionality-health")
    async def get_functionality_health(
        environment: OperationsEnvironment | None = None,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await functionality_health_service.get_health(
            environment=environment
        )

    @router.post(
        "/functionality/{functionality_id}/stop",
        dependencies=[Depends(require_permission(Permission.FUNCTIONALITY_CONTROL))],
    )
    def stop_functionality(
        functionality_id: str,
        request: FunctionalityStopRequest,
        principal: Principal = Depends(
            require_permission(Permission.FUNCTIONALITY_CONTROL)
        ),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        if functionality_control_service is None:
            raise _control_unavailable()

        try:
            result = functionality_control_service.stop(
                actor=principal,
                actor_name=principal.user_id,
                environment=request.environment.value,
                functionality_id=functionality_id,
                reason=request.reason,
            )
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
                detail=str(exc),
            ) from exc
        except PermissionError:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
                detail="Insufficient permission.",
            ) from None

        return _result_as_dict(result)

    @router.post(
        "/functionality/{functionality_id}/resume",
        dependencies=[Depends(require_permission(Permission.FUNCTIONALITY_CONTROL))],
    )
    def resume_functionality(
        functionality_id: str,
        request: FunctionalityResumeRequest,
        principal: Principal = Depends(
            require_permission(Permission.FUNCTIONALITY_CONTROL)
        ),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        if functionality_control_service is None:
            raise _control_unavailable()

        try:
            result = functionality_control_service.resume(
                actor=principal,
                actor_name=principal.user_id,
                environment=request.environment.value,
                functionality_id=functionality_id,
            )
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
                detail=str(exc),
            ) from exc
        except PermissionError:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
                detail="Insufficient permission.",
            ) from None

        return _result_as_dict(result)



    @router.get(
        "/platform-intelligence/reviews",
        dependencies=[Depends(require_permission(Permission.INTERNAL_DATA_READ))],
    )
    def get_platform_intelligence_reviews() -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            return platform_intelligence_review_api.list_reviews()
        except PlatformIntelligenceReviewNotConfigured as exc:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
                detail=str(exc),
            ) from None

    @router.get(
        "/platform-intelligence/reviews/{review_item_id}",
        dependencies=[Depends(require_permission(Permission.INTERNAL_DATA_READ))],
    )
    def get_platform_intelligence_review(
        review_item_id: str,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            result = platform_intelligence_review_api.get_review(review_item_id)
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
                detail=str(exc),
            ) from None
        except PlatformIntelligenceReviewNotConfigured as exc:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
                detail=str(exc),
            ) from None
        if result is None:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
                detail="Platform Intelligence review item not found.",
            )
        return result

    @router.get(
        "/platform-intelligence/escalations",
        dependencies=[Depends(require_permission(Permission.INTERNAL_DATA_READ))],
    )
    def get_platform_intelligence_escalations() -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            return platform_intelligence_review_api.list_escalations()
        except PlatformIntelligenceReviewNotConfigured as exc:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
                detail=str(exc),
            ) from None

    @router.get("/operational-events")
    def get_operational_events() -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        incidents = event_service.list_incidents()
        return [
            event.as_dict()
            for incident in incidents
            for event in incident.events
        ]

    @router.get("/provider-usage")
    def get_provider_usage() -> dict[str, Any]:
        if provider_usage_dashboard_service is None:
            return {
                "status": "not_configured",
                "reason": "provider_usage_dashboard_service_not_configured",
            }
        return provider_usage_dashboard_service.get_snapshot()

    return router


router = create_operations_center_router()

__all__ = ["router", "create_operations_center_router"]

from __future__ import annotations

from uuid import UUID

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Request, status

from app.api.dependencies.authorization import require_permission
from app.api.routes.developer_admin_transaction_contracts import (
    DeveloperAdminApprovalResponse,
    DeveloperAdminCreateTransactionRequest,
    DeveloperAdminExecuteTransactionResponse,
    DeveloperAdminPendingTransactionResponse,
)
from app.application.developer_admin.developer_admin_service import (
    DeveloperAdminLastAdminError,
    DeveloperAdminService,
    DeveloperAdminUserInactiveError,
    DeveloperAdminUserNotFoundError,
)
from app.application.developer_admin.developer_admin_transaction_service import (
    DeveloperAdminTransactionService,
)
from app.application.developer_admin.trusted_approval_provider import (
    TrustedApprovalRequest,
    TrustedApprovalUnavailableError,
    UnavailableTrustedApprovalProvider,
)
from app.application.identity.transaction_authorization import (
    TransactionAuthorizationApplicationError,
)
from app.domain.authorization.permissions import Permission
from app.domain.identity.principal import Principal


def _get_service(request: Request) -> DeveloperAdminService:
    service = getattr(request.app.state, "developer_admin_service", None)
    if service is None:
        raise RuntimeError("developer_admin_service is not configured.")
    return service


def _get_transaction_service(
    request: Request,
) -> DeveloperAdminTransactionService:
    service = getattr(
        request.app.state,
        "developer_admin_transaction_service",
        None,
    )
    if service is None:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "developer_admin_transaction_service is not configured."
        )
    return service


def _get_approval_provider(request: Request):
    return getattr(
        request.app.state,
        "developer_admin_trusted_approval_provider",
        UnavailableTrustedApprovalProvider(),
    )


router = APIRouter(
    prefix="/api/operations-center/developer-administrators",
    tags=["operations-center-developer-administrators"],
)


@router.get("")
async def list_developer_administrators(
    request: Request,
    _principal: Principal = Depends(
        require_permission(Permission.DEVELOPER_ADMIN_MANAGE)
    ),
):
    administrators = await _get_service(request).list_developer_administrators()

    return {
        "administrators": [
            {
                "user_id": item.user_id,
                "email": item.email,
                "username": item.username,
                "status": item.status,
                "email_verified": item.email_verified,
            }
            for item in administrators
        ]
    }


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Legacy collection mutation retirement boundary
#
# Direct Developer Administrator mutations are no longer supported.
#
# The only supported mutation path is:
#
#   create transaction
#       ->
#   trusted approval
#       ->
#   execute transaction
#
# This explicit POST tombstone exists only for the former collection
# mutation endpoint. It performs no mutation.
#
# The former DELETE /{user_id} endpoint intentionally does NOT exist.
# Keeping that route out of the router is part of the API contract.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


@router.post("")
async def legacy_direct_create_developer_administrator() -> None:
    raise HTTPException(
        status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
        detail=(
            "Developer Administrator direct mutation endpoint "
            "is no longer available."
        ),
    )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Transaction-authorized Developer Admin mutations
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


@router.post(
    "/transactions/grant",
    response_model=DeveloperAdminPendingTransactionResponse,
    status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
)
async def create_grant_transaction(
    payload: DeveloperAdminCreateTransactionRequest,
    request: Request,
    principal: Principal = Depends(
        require_permission(Permission.DEVELOPER_ADMIN_MANAGE)
    ),
) -> DeveloperAdminPendingTransactionResponse:
    try:
        authorization = _get_transaction_service(request).create_grant_transaction(
            principal=principal,
            target_user_id=payload.target_user_id,
        )
    except (
        PermissionError,
        TransactionAuthorizationApplicationError,
        ValueError,
    ) as exc:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
            detail=str(exc),
        ) from None

    transaction = authorization.transaction
    risk_context = dict(transaction.risk_context)

    return DeveloperAdminPendingTransactionResponse(
        authorization_id=authorization.authorization_id,
        transaction_id=transaction.transaction_id,
        operation=transaction.operation,
        audience=risk_context["audience"],
        target_user_id=UUID(risk_context["target_user_id"]),
        state=authorization.state.value,
        expires_at=authorization.expires_at,
    )


@router.post(
    "/transactions/revoke",
    response_model=DeveloperAdminPendingTransactionResponse,
    status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
)
async def create_revoke_transaction(
    payload: DeveloperAdminCreateTransactionRequest,
    request: Request,
    principal: Principal = Depends(
        require_permission(Permission.DEVELOPER_ADMIN_MANAGE)
    ),
) -> DeveloperAdminPendingTransactionResponse:
    try:
        authorization = _get_transaction_service(request).create_revoke_transaction(
            principal=principal,
            target_user_id=payload.target_user_id,
        )
    except (
        PermissionError,
        TransactionAuthorizationApplicationError,
        ValueError,
    ) as exc:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
            detail=str(exc),
        ) from None

    transaction = authorization.transaction
    risk_context = dict(transaction.risk_context)

    return DeveloperAdminPendingTransactionResponse(
        authorization_id=authorization.authorization_id,
        transaction_id=transaction.transaction_id,
        operation=transaction.operation,
        audience=risk_context["audience"],
        target_user_id=UUID(risk_context["target_user_id"]),
        state=authorization.state.value,
        expires_at=authorization.expires_at,
    )


@router.post(
    "/transactions/{authorization_id}/approve",
    response_model=DeveloperAdminApprovalResponse,
)
async def approve_transaction(
    authorization_id: str,
    request: Request,
    principal: Principal = Depends(
        require_permission(Permission.DEVELOPER_ADMIN_MANAGE)
    ),
) -> DeveloperAdminApprovalResponse:
    transaction_service = _get_transaction_service(request)

    authorization = transaction_service.get_authorization(
        authorization_id=authorization_id
    )

    if authorization is None:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
            detail="Transaction authorization was not found.",
        )

    provider = _get_approval_provider(request)

    try:
        approval = await provider.approve(
            request=TrustedApprovalRequest(
                authorization_id=authorization.authorization_id,
                principal_id=principal.user_id,
                audience=transaction_service.AUDIENCE,
                transaction_id=authorization.transaction.transaction_id,
                requested_at=authorization.created_at,
            )
        )

        approved = transaction_service.attach_trusted_approval(
            principal=principal,
            approval=approval,
        )
    except TrustedApprovalUnavailableError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
            detail=str(exc),
        ) from None
    except (
        PermissionError,
        TransactionAuthorizationApplicationError,
        ValueError,
    ) as exc:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
            detail=str(exc),
        ) from None

    return DeveloperAdminApprovalResponse(
        authorization_id=approved.authorization_id,
        transaction_id=approved.transaction.transaction_id,
        state=approved.state.value,
        expires_at=approved.expires_at,
    )


@router.post(
    "/transactions/{authorization_id}/execute",
    response_model=DeveloperAdminExecuteTransactionResponse,
)
async def execute_transaction(
    authorization_id: str,
    request: Request,
    principal: Principal = Depends(
        require_permission(Permission.DEVELOPER_ADMIN_MANAGE)
    ),
) -> DeveloperAdminExecuteTransactionResponse:
    transaction_service = _get_transaction_service(request)

    authorization = transaction_service.get_authorization(
        authorization_id=authorization_id
    )

    if authorization is None:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
            detail="Transaction authorization was not found.",
        )

    try:
        transaction = authorization.transaction

        if transaction.operation == transaction_service.GRANT_OPERATION:
            await transaction_service.execute_grant(
                principal=principal,
                authorization_id=authorization_id,
                transaction=transaction,
            )
        elif transaction.operation == transaction_service.REVOKE_OPERATION:
            await transaction_service.execute_revoke(
                principal=principal,
                authorization_id=authorization_id,
                transaction=transaction,
            )
        else:
            raise PermissionError(
                "Developer Administrator transaction operation is invalid."
            )

    except DeveloperAdminUserNotFoundError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
            detail=str(exc),
        ) from None
    except (
        DeveloperAdminUserInactiveError,
        DeveloperAdminLastAdminError,
    ) as exc:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT,
            detail=str(exc),
        ) from None
    except (
        PermissionError,
        TransactionAuthorizationApplicationError,
        ValueError,
    ) as exc:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
            detail=str(exc),
        ) from None

    consumed = transaction_service.get_authorization(
        authorization_id=authorization_id
    )

    state = (
        consumed.state.value
        if consumed is not None
        else "CONSUMED"
    )

    return DeveloperAdminExecuteTransactionResponse(
        authorization_id=authorization_id,
        state=state,
    )


__all__ = ["router"]

============================================================
FILE: app\application\developer_admin\developer_admin_service.py
============================================================
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from uuid import UUID

from app.application.developer_admin.developer_admin_audit import (
    DeveloperAdminAuditService,
)
from app.application.developer_admin.developer_admin_uow import (
    DeveloperAdminUnitOfWork,
)
from app.application.developer_admin.developer_admin_uow_factory import (
    DeveloperAdminUnitOfWorkFactory,
)
from app.domain.authorization.permissions import Role
from app.domain.identity.principal import Principal
from app.infrastructure.auth.postgresql_auth_repository import (
    AuthUserRecord,
    PostgreSQLAuthenticationRepository,
)


class DeveloperAdminError(Exception):
    """Base error for Developer Administration operations."""


class DeveloperAdminUserNotFoundError(DeveloperAdminError):
    """Requested target user does not exist."""


class DeveloperAdminUserInactiveError(DeveloperAdminError):
    """Requested target user cannot receive Developer Admin privileges."""


class DeveloperAdminLastAdminError(DeveloperAdminError):
    """The final Developer Administrator cannot be removed."""


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class DeveloperAdminRecord:
    user_id: UUID
    email: str
    username: str
    status: str
    email_verified: bool


class DeveloperAdminService:
    """Application boundary for controlled Developer Administrator management.

    Production composition injects a DeveloperAdminUnitOfWorkFactory.
    Each Developer Administrator mutation obtains a fresh Unit-of-Work
    instance from that factory so role mutations and their security audit
    evidence share one database transaction without sharing mutable UoW
    state between operations.

    The repository-only constructor path is retained for compatibility with
    existing isolated service tests. That compatibility path is deliberately
    not used by the production application.
    """

    ROLE = Role.DEVELOPER_ADMIN.value

    def __init__(
        self,
        repository: PostgreSQLAuthenticationRepository,
        *,
        unit_of_work_factory: DeveloperAdminUnitOfWorkFactory | None = None,
        audit_service: DeveloperAdminAuditService | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        if repository is None:
            raise ValueError("repository is required.")

        if (unit_of_work_factory is None) != (audit_service is None):
            raise ValueError(
                "unit_of_work_factory and audit_service must be supplied together."
            )

        self._repository = repository
        self._unit_of_work_factory = unit_of_work_factory
        self._audit_service = audit_service

    async def list_developer_administrators(
        self,
    ) -> tuple[DeveloperAdminRecord, ...]:
        users = await self._repository.get_users_with_role(
            role=self.ROLE,
        )

        return tuple(
            self._to_record(user)
            for user in users
        )

    async def grant_developer_admin(
        self,
        *,
        user_id: UUID,
        actor: Principal | None = None,
        correlation_id: str | None = None,
    ) -> DeveloperAdminRecord:
        # Production path: role mutation + audit share one transaction.
        if self._unit_of_work_factory is not None:
            if actor is None:
                raise ValueError(
                    "actor is required when using the atomic Developer Admin path."
                )

            return await self._grant_atomically(
                actor=actor,
                user_id=user_id,
                correlation_id=correlation_id,
            )

        # Compatibility path for existing isolated application tests.
        # Production composition never uses this path.
        return await self._grant_legacy(
            user_id=user_id,
        )

    async def revoke_developer_admin(
        self,
        *,
        user_id: UUID,
        actor: Principal | None = None,
        correlation_id: str | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        # Production path: role mutation + audit share one transaction.
        if self._unit_of_work_factory is not None:
            if actor is None:
                raise ValueError(
                    "actor is required when using the atomic Developer Admin path."
                )

            await self._revoke_atomically(
                actor=actor,
                user_id=user_id,
                correlation_id=correlation_id,
            )
            return

        # Compatibility path for existing isolated application tests.
        # Production composition never uses this path.
        await self._revoke_legacy(
            user_id=user_id,
        )

    async def _grant_atomically(
        self,
        *,
        actor: Principal,
        user_id: UUID,
        correlation_id: str | None,
    ) -> DeveloperAdminRecord:
        assert self._unit_of_work_factory is not None
        assert self._audit_service is not None

        # A fresh UoW is created for every Developer Admin operation.
        async with self._unit_of_work_factory.create() as uow:
            target = await uow.get_user(user_id)

            if target is None:
                raise DeveloperAdminUserNotFoundError(
                    "Target user does not exist."
                )

            if target.status != "active" or not target.email_verified:
                raise DeveloperAdminUserInactiveError(
                    "Developer Administrator privileges require an active, "
                    "verified user."
                )

            created = await uow.add_user_role(
                user_id=user_id,
                role=self.ROLE,
            )

            if created:
                event = await self._audit_service.build_event(
                    event_type=DeveloperAdminAuditService.GRANTED,
                    actor_user_id=UUID(actor.user_id),
                    target_user_id=target.id,
                    actor_email=actor.email,
                    target_email=target.email,
                    correlation_id=correlation_id,
                    metadata={"operation": "grant"},
                )

                await uow.record_audit(event)

            return self._to_record(target)

    async def _revoke_atomically(
        self,
        *,
        actor: Principal,
        user_id: UUID,
        correlation_id: str | None,
    ) -> None:
        assert self._unit_of_work_factory is not None
        assert self._audit_service is not None

        # A fresh UoW is created for every Developer Admin operation.
        async with self._unit_of_work_factory.create() as uow:
            target = await uow.get_user(user_id)

            if target is None:
                raise DeveloperAdminUserNotFoundError(
                    "Target user does not exist."
                )

            roles = await uow.get_user_roles(user_id)

            if self.ROLE not in roles:
                return

            current_count = await uow.count_users_with_role(
                role=self.ROLE,
            )

            if current_count <= 1:
                raise DeveloperAdminLastAdminError(
                    "The last Developer Administrator cannot be removed."
                )

            removed = await uow.remove_user_role(
                user_id=user_id,
                role=self.ROLE,
            )

            if removed:
                event = await self._audit_service.build_event(
                    event_type=DeveloperAdminAuditService.REVOKED,
                    actor_user_id=UUID(actor.user_id),
                    target_user_id=target.id,
                    actor_email=actor.email,
                    target_email=target.email,
                    correlation_id=correlation_id,
                    metadata={"operation": "revoke"},
                )

                await uow.record_audit(event)

    async def _grant_legacy(
        self,
        *,
        user_id: UUID,
    ) -> DeveloperAdminRecord:
        user = await self._repository.get_user_for_identity(user_id)

        if user is None:
            raise DeveloperAdminUserNotFoundError(
                "Target user does not exist."
            )

        if user.status != "active" or not user.email_verified:
            raise DeveloperAdminUserInactiveError(
                "Developer Administrator privileges require an active, "
                "verified user."
            )

        await self._repository.add_user_role(
            user_id=user_id,
            role=self.ROLE,
        )

        return self._to_record(user)

    async def _revoke_legacy(
        self,
        *,
        user_id: UUID,
    ) -> None:
        user = await self._repository.get_user_for_identity(user_id)

        if user is None:
            raise DeveloperAdminUserNotFoundError(
                "Target user does not exist."
            )

        current_count = await self._repository.count_users_with_role(
            role=self.ROLE,
        )

        roles = await self._repository.get_user_roles(user_id)

        if self.ROLE not in roles:
            return

        if current_count <= 1:
            raise DeveloperAdminLastAdminError(
                "The last Developer Administrator cannot be removed."
            )

        await self._repository.remove_user_role(
            user_id=user_id,
            role=self.ROLE,
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _to_record(
        user: AuthUserRecord,
    ) -> DeveloperAdminRecord:
        return DeveloperAdminRecord(
            user_id=user.id,
            email=user.email,
            username=user.username,
            status=user.status,
            email_verified=user.email_verified,
        )


__all__ = [
    "DeveloperAdminError",
    "DeveloperAdminLastAdminError",
    "DeveloperAdminRecord",
    "DeveloperAdminService",
    "DeveloperAdminUserInactiveError",
    "DeveloperAdminUserNotFoundError",
]

============================================================
FILE: app\application\developer_admin\developer_admin_transaction_service.py
============================================================
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from typing import Protocol
from uuid import UUID, uuid4

from app.application.developer_admin.developer_admin_service import (
    DeveloperAdminRecord,
    DeveloperAdminService,
)
from app.application.identity.transaction_authorization import (
    TransactionAuthorizationApplicationError,
    TransactionAuthorizationApplicationService,
)
from app.domain.identity.authentication_assurance import (
    AuthenticationAssuranceLevel,
)
from app.domain.identity.principal import Principal
from app.domain.identity.transaction_authorization import (
    CanonicalTransaction,
    TransactionAuthorization,
    TransactionAuthorizationDecision,
    TrustedApprovalEvidence,
)


class TrustedClock(Protocol):
    """Trusted application/server clock."""

    def now(self) -> datetime:
        ...


class DeveloperAdminTransactionService:
    """Application boundary for authorized Developer Admin mutations.

    Security flow:

        authenticated principal
            -> exact Developer Admin transaction
            -> pending user approval
            -> trusted assurance / approval
            -> exact transaction binding
            -> single-use consumption
            -> Developer Admin mutation

    The target is always extracted from the authorized canonical transaction.
    No execution request is allowed to replace the authorized target.
    """

    AUDIENCE = "easychatbot.developer_admin"

    GRANT_OPERATION = "developer_admin.grant"
    REVOKE_OPERATION = "developer_admin.revoke"

    REQUIRED_ASSURANCE_LEVEL = AuthenticationAssuranceLevel.A2
    DEFAULT_TRANSACTION_TTL = timedelta(minutes=5)

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        developer_admin_service: DeveloperAdminService,
        transaction_authorization_service: (
            TransactionAuthorizationApplicationService
        ),
        clock: TrustedClock,
    ) -> None:
        if developer_admin_service is None:
            raise ValueError("developer_admin_service is required.")

        if transaction_authorization_service is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "transaction_authorization_service is required."
            )

        if clock is None:
            raise ValueError("clock is required.")

        self._developer_admin_service = developer_admin_service
        self._transaction_authorization_service = (
            transaction_authorization_service
        )
        self._clock = clock

    def create_grant_transaction(
        self,
        *,
        principal: Principal,
        target_user_id: UUID,
        created_at: datetime | None = None,
        expires_at: datetime | None = None,
    ) -> TransactionAuthorization:
        return self._create_transaction(
            principal=principal,
            target_user_id=target_user_id,
            operation=self.GRANT_OPERATION,
            risk_operation="grant",
            created_at=created_at,
            expires_at=expires_at,
        )

    def create_revoke_transaction(
        self,
        *,
        principal: Principal,
        target_user_id: UUID,
        created_at: datetime | None = None,
        expires_at: datetime | None = None,
    ) -> TransactionAuthorization:
        return self._create_transaction(
            principal=principal,
            target_user_id=target_user_id,
            operation=self.REVOKE_OPERATION,
            risk_operation="revoke",
            created_at=created_at,
            expires_at=expires_at,
        )

    def _create_transaction(
        self,
        *,
        principal: Principal,
        target_user_id: UUID,
        operation: str,
        risk_operation: str,
        created_at: datetime | None,
        expires_at: datetime | None,
    ) -> TransactionAuthorization:
        self._require_valid_principal(principal)

        if not isinstance(target_user_id, UUID):
            raise ValueError("target_user_id must be a UUID.")

        effective_created_at = (
            created_at
            if created_at is not None
            else self._clock.now()
        )

        effective_expires_at = (
            expires_at
            if expires_at is not None
            else effective_created_at + self.DEFAULT_TRANSACTION_TTL
        )

        self._validate_transaction_times(
            created_at=effective_created_at,
            expires_at=effective_expires_at,
        )

        transaction = CanonicalTransaction.from_mapping(
            transaction_id=str(uuid4()),
            principal_id=principal.user_id,
            amount="0.00",
            currency="N/A",
            merchant_or_service="easychatbot",
            operation=operation,
            timestamp=effective_created_at,
            nonce=str(uuid4()),
            risk_context={
                "target_user_id": str(target_user_id),
                "operation": risk_operation,
                "audience": self.AUDIENCE,
            },
        )

        return (
            self._transaction_authorization_service.create_pending_authorization(
                principal=principal,
                transaction=transaction,
                authorization_id=str(uuid4()),
                created_at=effective_created_at,
                expires_at=effective_expires_at,
            )
        )

    def get_authorization(
        self,
        *,
        authorization_id: str,
    ) -> TransactionAuthorization | None:
        if not authorization_id:
            return None

        return self._transaction_authorization_service.registry.get(
            authorization_id
        )

    def attach_trusted_approval(
        self,
        *,
        principal: Principal,
        approval: TrustedApprovalEvidence,
    ) -> TransactionAuthorization:
        authorization = self.get_authorization(
            authorization_id=approval.authorization_id,
        )

        if authorization is None:
            raise TransactionAuthorizationApplicationError(
                "Transaction authorization was not found."
            )

        return self._transaction_authorization_service.attach_user_approval(
            principal=principal,
            authorization=authorization,
            approval=approval,
            clock=self._clock,
        )

    async def execute_grant(
        self,
        *,
        principal: Principal,
        authorization_id: str,
        transaction: CanonicalTransaction | None = None,
        clock: TrustedClock | None = None,
    ) -> DeveloperAdminRecord:
        authorization = self._resolve_execution_authorization(
            authorization_id=authorization_id,
            transaction=transaction,
            expected_operation=self.GRANT_OPERATION,
        )

        result = self._consume(
            principal=principal,
            authorization=authorization,
            clock=clock,
        )

        if result.decision is not TransactionAuthorizationDecision.ALLOW:
            raise PermissionError("transaction authorization denied")

        target_user_id = self._target_user_id_from_transaction(
            authorization.transaction,
            expected_operation=self.GRANT_OPERATION,
            expected_risk_operation="grant",
        )

        return await self._developer_admin_service.grant_developer_admin(
            actor=principal,
            user_id=target_user_id,
        )

    async def execute_revoke(
        self,
        *,
        principal: Principal,
        authorization_id: str,
        transaction: CanonicalTransaction | None = None,
        clock: TrustedClock | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        authorization = self._resolve_execution_authorization(
            authorization_id=authorization_id,
            transaction=transaction,
            expected_operation=self.REVOKE_OPERATION,
        )

        result = self._consume(
            principal=principal,
            authorization=authorization,
            clock=clock,
        )

        if result.decision is not TransactionAuthorizationDecision.ALLOW:
            raise PermissionError("transaction authorization denied")

        target_user_id = self._target_user_id_from_transaction(
            authorization.transaction,
            expected_operation=self.REVOKE_OPERATION,
            expected_risk_operation="revoke",
        )

        await self._developer_admin_service.revoke_developer_admin(
            actor=principal,
            user_id=target_user_id,
        )

    def _consume(
        self,
        *,
        principal: Principal,
        authorization: TransactionAuthorization,
        clock: TrustedClock | None,
    ):
        self._require_valid_principal(principal)

        return self._transaction_authorization_service.consume_authorization(
            principal=principal,
            authorization_id=authorization.authorization_id,
            expected_transaction=authorization.transaction,
            clock=clock if clock is not None else self._clock,
            required_assurance_level=self.REQUIRED_ASSURANCE_LEVEL,
        )

    def _resolve_execution_authorization(
        self,
        *,
        authorization_id: str,
        transaction: CanonicalTransaction | None,
        expected_operation: str,
    ) -> TransactionAuthorization:
        if not authorization_id:
            raise TransactionAuthorizationApplicationError(
                "Transaction authorization ID is required."
            )

        authorization = self.get_authorization(
            authorization_id=authorization_id,
        )

        if authorization is None:
            raise TransactionAuthorizationApplicationError(
                "Transaction authorization was not found."
            )

        if transaction is not None:
            if not isinstance(transaction, CanonicalTransaction):
                raise TransactionAuthorizationApplicationError(
                    "Canonical transaction is required."
                )

            if (
                transaction.canonical_representation()
                != authorization.transaction.canonical_representation()
            ):
                raise TransactionAuthorizationApplicationError(
                    "Canonical transaction binding mismatch."
                )

        self._target_user_id_from_transaction(
            authorization.transaction,
            expected_operation=expected_operation,
            expected_risk_operation=(
                "grant"
                if expected_operation == self.GRANT_OPERATION
                else "revoke"
            ),
        )

        return authorization

    def _target_user_id_from_transaction(
        self,
        transaction: CanonicalTransaction,
        *,
        expected_operation: str | None = None,
        expected_risk_operation: str | None = None,
    ) -> UUID:
        """Extract and validate the target exclusively from the transaction.

        When no expected operation is supplied, this helper defaults to the
        grant operation because the target-extraction contract is defined
        against the standard Developer Admin grant transaction.

        Execution paths always supply the explicit expected operation and
        risk operation, so grant and revoke cannot be substituted for one
        another.
        """
        if not isinstance(transaction, CanonicalTransaction):
            raise PermissionError("transaction is invalid")

        operation = (
            expected_operation
            if expected_operation is not None
            else self.GRANT_OPERATION
        )

        risk_operation = (
            expected_risk_operation
            if expected_risk_operation is not None
            else "grant"
        )

        if transaction.operation != operation:
            raise PermissionError(
                "transaction operation is invalid"
            )

        risk_context = dict(transaction.risk_context)

        if risk_context.get("operation") != risk_operation:
            raise PermissionError(
                "transaction risk operation is invalid"
            )

        if risk_context.get("audience") != self.AUDIENCE:
            raise PermissionError(
                "transaction audience is invalid"
            )

        target_value = risk_context.get("target_user_id")

        if not target_value:
            raise PermissionError(
                "transaction target is invalid"
            )

        try:
            return UUID(str(target_value))
        except (TypeError, ValueError, AttributeError) as exc:
            raise PermissionError(
                "transaction target is invalid"
            ) from exc

    @staticmethod
    def _require_valid_principal(
        principal: Principal,
    ) -> None:
        if principal is None:
            raise PermissionError(
                "authenticated principal is required"
            )

        if not principal.user_id:
            raise PermissionError(
                "authenticated principal is invalid"
            )

        if not principal.email_verified:
            raise PermissionError(
                "authenticated principal email is not verified"
            )

        account_status = getattr(
            principal,
            "account_status",
            None,
        )

        status_value = getattr(
            account_status,
            "value",
            account_status,
        )

        if status_value != "active":
            raise PermissionError(
                "authenticated principal is not active"
            )

    @staticmethod
    def _validate_transaction_times(
        *,
        created_at: datetime,
        expires_at: datetime,
    ) -> None:
        if created_at.tzinfo is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "created_at must be timezone-aware"
            )

        if expires_at.tzinfo is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "expires_at must be timezone-aware"
            )

        if expires_at <= created_at:
            raise ValueError(
                "expires_at must be later than created_at"
            )


__all__ = ["DeveloperAdminTransactionService"]

FILE: app\application\operations_center\platform_intelligence_retry_worker.py
