"""Contracts for the bounded PI escalation retry worker."""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime
from enum import StrEnum


class PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemStatus(StrEnum):
    SUCCEEDED = "SUCCEEDED"
    SKIPPED = "SKIPPED"
    FAILED = "FAILED"


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemResult:
    notification_key: str
    status: PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemStatus
    reason: str
    attempt: int | None = None
    provider_message_id: str | None = None
    error_type: str | None = None

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not self.notification_key.strip():
            raise ValueError("notification_key must be nonblank.")
        if not self.reason.strip():
            raise ValueError("reason must be nonblank.")
        if self.attempt is not None and self.attempt < 1:
            raise ValueError("attempt must be at least 1.")
        if self.error_type is not None and not self.error_type.strip():
            raise ValueError("error_type must be nonblank when provided.")


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerResult:
    requested_limit: int
    discovered_count: int
    processed_count: int
    succeeded_count: int
    skipped_count: int
    failed_count: int
    items: tuple[PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemResult, ...]

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if self.requested_limit < 1:
            raise ValueError("requested_limit must be at least 1.")
        if self.discovered_count < 0:
            raise ValueError("discovered_count cannot be negative.")
        if self.processed_count < 0:
            raise ValueError("processed_count cannot be negative.")
        if self.processed_count != len(self.items):
            raise ValueError("processed_count must equal the number of items.")
        if self.succeeded_count < 0 or self.skipped_count < 0 or self.failed_count < 0:
            raise ValueError("result counts cannot be negative.")
        if (
            self.succeeded_count + self.skipped_count + self.failed_count
            != self.processed_count
        ):
            raise ValueError("result counts must equal processed_count.")
        if self.discovered_count > self.requested_limit:
            raise ValueError("discovered_count cannot exceed requested_limit.")
        keys = [item.notification_key for item in self.items]
        if len(keys) != len(set(keys)):
            raise ValueError("worker result cannot contain duplicate notification keys.")


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerRequest:
    limit: int = 100

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if self.limit < 1:
            raise ValueError("limit must be at least 1.")


============================================================
FILE: app\application\operations_center\platform_intelligence_retry_worker_service.py
============================================================
"""Bounded, scheduler-free orchestration boundary for PI retry work."""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_candidate_discovery import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidateDiscoveryService,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_orchestration import (
    PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunStatus,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_orchestrator import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryOrchestrator,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_retry_worker import (
    PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemResult,
    PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemStatus,
    PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerRequest,
    PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerResult,
)


class PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorker:
    """Process one bounded candidate batch through the governed retry chain.

    This class deliberately does not:
    - schedule itself;
    - sleep;
    - create background tasks;
    - access providers;
    - implement retry policy;
    - claim candidates directly;
    - mutate risk or human-review state.

    The caller owns invocation timing and supplies the reference timestamp.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        discovery_service: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidateDiscoveryService,
        orchestrator: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryOrchestrator,
    ) -> None:
        if discovery_service is None:
            raise ValueError("discovery_service cannot be None.")

        if orchestrator is None:
            raise ValueError("orchestrator cannot be None.")

        self._discovery_service = discovery_service
        self._orchestrator = orchestrator

    async def run(
        self,
        *,
        request: PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerRequest,
        reference_at: datetime,
    ) -> PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerResult:
        if reference_at.tzinfo is None or reference_at.utcoffset() is None:
            raise ValueError("reference_at must be timezone-aware.")

        batch = await self._discovery_service.discover(
            limit=request.limit,
        )

        results: list[PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemResult] = []

        for candidate in batch.candidates:
            try:
                execution = await self._orchestrator.run_candidate(
                    candidate=candidate,
                    reference_at=reference_at,
                )

            except Exception as exc:
                results.append(
                    PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemResult(
                        notification_key=candidate.notification_key,
                        status=(
                            PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemStatus
                            .FAILED
                        ),
                        reason="CANDIDATE_EXECUTION_ERROR",
                        attempt=candidate.attempt,
                        error_type=type(exc).__name__,
                    )
                )
                continue

            if (
                execution.status
                is PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunStatus.SUCCEEDED
            ):
                results.append(
                    PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemResult(
                        notification_key=execution.notification_key,
                        status=(
                            PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemStatus
                            .SUCCEEDED
                        ),
                        reason=execution.reason,
                        attempt=execution.attempt,
                        provider_message_id=(
                            execution.provider_message_id
                        ),
                    )
                )

            elif (
                execution.status
                is PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunStatus.SKIPPED
            ):
                results.append(
                    PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemResult(
                        notification_key=execution.notification_key,
                        status=(
                            PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemStatus
                            .SKIPPED
                        ),
                        reason=execution.reason,
                        attempt=execution.attempt,
                    )
                )

            else:
                results.append(
                    PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemResult(
                        notification_key=execution.notification_key,
                        status=(
                            PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemStatus
                            .FAILED
                        ),
                        reason=execution.reason,
                        attempt=execution.attempt,
                    )
                )

        succeeded = sum(
            item.status
            is PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemStatus.SUCCEEDED
            for item in results
        )

        skipped = sum(
            item.status
            is PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemStatus.SKIPPED
            for item in results
        )

        failed = sum(
            item.status
            is PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemStatus.FAILED
            for item in results
        )

        return PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerResult(
            requested_limit=request.limit,
            discovered_count=len(batch.candidates),
            processed_count=len(results),
            succeeded_count=succeeded,
            skipped_count=skipped,
            failed_count=failed,
            items=tuple(results),
        )

============================================================
FILE: app\application\operations_center\platform_intelligence_escalation_retry.py
============================================================
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from enum import StrEnum
from typing import FrozenSet

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryRecord,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus,
)


class PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyStatus(StrEnum):
    DRAFT = "DRAFT"
    APPROVED = "APPROVED"


class PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision(StrEnum):
    ELIGIBLE = "ELIGIBLE"
    NOT_ELIGIBLE = "NOT_ELIGIBLE"


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicy:
    """Versioned, explicitly approved retry policy for PI escalation delivery."""

    policy_id: str
    policy_version: str
    status: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyStatus
    max_attempts: int
    base_backoff_seconds: int
    max_backoff_seconds: int
    retryable_error_codes: FrozenSet[str]
    retry_interrupted_started: bool = True

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        required = (self.policy_id, self.policy_version)

        if any(
            not isinstance(value, str) or not value.strip()
            for value in required
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "policy_id and policy_version cannot be blank."
            )

        if self.max_attempts < 1:
            raise ValueError(
                "max_attempts must be at least 1."
            )

        if self.base_backoff_seconds < 0:
            raise ValueError(
                "base_backoff_seconds cannot be negative."
            )

        if self.max_backoff_seconds < self.base_backoff_seconds:
            raise ValueError(
                "max_backoff_seconds cannot be lower than "
                "base_backoff_seconds."
            )

        if any(
            not isinstance(code, str) or not code.strip()
            for code in self.retryable_error_codes
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "retryable_error_codes cannot contain blank values."
            )


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryResult:
    """Deterministic retry eligibility decision and policy-derived timing."""

    decision: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision
    notification_key: str
    attempt: int
    policy_id: str
    policy_version: str
    reason: str
    next_eligible_at: datetime | None = None
    backoff_seconds: int | None = None


class PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService:
    """Evaluate whether a durable PI delivery record may be retried.

    This service is side-effect free.

    It does not:
    - mutate delivery state;
    - call an email provider;
    - schedule work;
    - read the wall clock.

    The caller supplies the reference time used for the eligibility decision.
    The delivery record supplies the authoritative timestamp from which the
    retry backoff is calculated.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        policy: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicy,
    ) -> None:
        if policy is None:
            raise ValueError("policy cannot be None.")

        self._policy = policy

    @property
    def policy(self) -> PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicy:
        return self._policy

    def evaluate(
        self,
        *,
        record: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryRecord,
        reference_at: datetime,
    ) -> PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryResult:
        self._validate_reference_time(reference_at)

        if not record.notification_key.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "notification_key cannot be blank."
            )

        if record.attempt < 1:
            raise ValueError(
                "delivery attempt must be at least 1."
            )

        if (
            self._policy.status
            is not PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyStatus.APPROVED
        ):
            return self._result(
                record=record,
                decision=(
                    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision
                    .NOT_ELIGIBLE
                ),
                reason="Retry policy is not APPROVED.",
            )

        if (
            record.status
            is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.SUCCEEDED
        ):
            return self._result(
                record=record,
                decision=(
                    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision
                    .NOT_ELIGIBLE
                ),
                reason="Delivery already SUCCEEDED.",
            )

        if record.attempt >= self._policy.max_attempts:
            return self._result(
                record=record,
                decision=(
                    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision
                    .NOT_ELIGIBLE
                ),
                reason="Maximum delivery attempts reached.",
            )

        if (
            record.status
            is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.FAILED
        ):
            if not record.error_code:
                return self._result(
                    record=record,
                    decision=(
                        PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision
                        .NOT_ELIGIBLE
                    ),
                    reason=(
                        "FAILED delivery has no error code for retry "
                        "classification."
                    ),
                )

            if (
                record.error_code
                not in self._policy.retryable_error_codes
            ):
                return self._result(
                    record=record,
                    decision=(
                        PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision
                        .NOT_ELIGIBLE
                    ),
                    reason=(
                        f"Error code {record.error_code!r} "
                        "is not retryable."
                    ),
                )

            return self._eligible_result(
                record=record,
                reference_at=reference_at,
            )

        if (
            record.status
            is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.STARTED
        ):
            if not self._policy.retry_interrupted_started:
                return self._result(
                    record=record,
                    decision=(
                        PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision
                        .NOT_ELIGIBLE
                    ),
                    reason=(
                        "Interrupted STARTED deliveries are not "
                        "retryable by policy."
                    ),
                )

            # STARTED represents recovery of an interrupted attempt.
            # Recovery does NOT increment the attempt, but it remains
            # subject to the same policy-controlled backoff.
            return self._eligible_result(
                record=record,
                reference_at=reference_at,
            )

        return self._result(
            record=record,
            decision=(
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision
                .NOT_ELIGIBLE
            ),
            reason=(
                f"Delivery status {record.status.value!r} "
                "is not retryable."
            ),
        )

    def backoff_seconds(self, *, attempt: int) -> int:
        """Return deterministic exponential backoff for the retry.

        Attempt 1:
            base_backoff_seconds

        Attempt 2:
            base_backoff_seconds * 2

        Attempt 3:
            base_backoff_seconds * 4

        The result is capped at max_backoff_seconds.
        """
        if attempt < 1:
            raise ValueError(
                "attempt must be at least 1."
            )

        multiplier = 2 ** (attempt - 1)

        return min(
            self._policy.base_backoff_seconds * multiplier,
            self._policy.max_backoff_seconds,
        )

    def _eligible_result(
        self,
        *,
        record: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryRecord,
        reference_at: datetime,
    ) -> PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryResult:
        delay = self.backoff_seconds(
            attempt=record.attempt
        )

        # The backoff is calculated from the persisted delivery timestamp,
        # not from the caller's current reference time.
        #
        # This is essential for deterministic due/not-due evaluation.
        if record.updated_at is None:
            return self._result(
                record=record,
                decision=(
                    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision
                    .NOT_ELIGIBLE
                ),
                reason=(
                    "Delivery record has no updated_at timestamp "
                    "from which retry timing can be established."
                ),
            )

        self._validate_timestamp(
            record.updated_at,
            field_name="record.updated_at",
        )

        next_eligible_at = (
            record.updated_at
            + timedelta(seconds=delay)
        )

        if reference_at < next_eligible_at:
            return self._result(
                record=record,
                decision=(
                    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision
                    .ELIGIBLE
                ),
                reason=(
                    "Delivery is eligible but the policy-derived "
                    "backoff window has not elapsed."
                ),
                next_eligible_at=next_eligible_at,
                backoff_seconds=delay,
            )

        return self._result(
            record=record,
            decision=(
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision
                .ELIGIBLE
            ),
            reason=(
                "Delivery is eligible for a policy-controlled retry."
            ),
            next_eligible_at=next_eligible_at,
            backoff_seconds=delay,
        )

    def _result(
        self,
        *,
        record: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryRecord,
        decision: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision,
        reason: str,
        next_eligible_at: datetime | None = None,
        backoff_seconds: int | None = None,
    ) -> PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryResult:
        return PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryResult(
            decision=decision,
            notification_key=record.notification_key,
            attempt=record.attempt,
            policy_id=self._policy.policy_id,
            policy_version=self._policy.policy_version,
            reason=reason,
            next_eligible_at=next_eligible_at,
            backoff_seconds=backoff_seconds,
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _validate_reference_time(
        reference_at: datetime,
    ) -> None:
        if (
            reference_at.tzinfo is None
            or reference_at.utcoffset() is None
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "reference_at must be timezone-aware."
            )

    @staticmethod
    def _validate_timestamp(
        value: datetime,
        *,
        field_name: str,
    ) -> None:
        if value.tzinfo is None or value.utcoffset() is None:
            raise ValueError(
                f"{field_name} must be timezone-aware."
            )

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Protocol, Sequence

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryRecord,
)


class PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidateRepository(Protocol):
    """Read-only application boundary for retry candidate discovery."""

    async def list_retry_candidates(
        self,
        *,
        limit: int,
    ) -> Sequence[PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryRecord]:
        """Return durable FAILED/STARTED delivery records for policy evaluation."""
        ...


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidate:
    """Durable delivery state selected for policy evaluation."""

    notification_key: str
    recommendation_id: str
    risk_decision_id: str
    review_item_id: str
    correlation_id: str
    attempt: int
    status: str
    error_code: str | None
    updated_at: datetime | None


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidateBatch:
    """Deterministic discovery result; no retry is executed here."""

    candidates: tuple[PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidate, ...]
    limit: int


============================================================
FILE: app\application\operations_center\platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_candidate_discovery.py
============================================================
from __future__ import annotations

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_candidates import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidate,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidateBatch,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidateRepository,
)


class PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidateDiscoveryService:
    """Discover durable retry work without executing it.

    This boundary deliberately does not:
    - call the retry policy;
    - mutate delivery state;
    - send email;
    - schedule work;
    - claim/lock candidates.

    Policy eligibility and execution remain separate steps.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        repository: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidateRepository,
    ) -> None:
        if repository is None:
            raise ValueError("repository cannot be None.")

        self._repository = repository

    async def discover(
        self,
        *,
        limit: int = 100,
    ) -> PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidateBatch:
        if limit < 1:
            raise ValueError("limit must be at least 1.")

        records = await self._repository.list_retry_candidates(
            limit=limit,
        )

        candidates: list[PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidate] = []

        for record in records:
            if record.status not in (
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.FAILED,
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.STARTED,
            ):
                continue

            candidates.append(
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidate(
                    notification_key=record.notification_key,
                    recommendation_id=record.recommendation_id,
                    risk_decision_id=record.risk_decision_id,
                    review_item_id=record.review_item_id,
                    correlation_id=record.correlation_id,
                    attempt=record.attempt,
                    status=record.status.value,
                    error_code=record.error_code,
                    updated_at=record.updated_at,
                )
            )

        candidates.sort(
            key=lambda candidate: (
                candidate.updated_at is None,
                candidate.updated_at,
                candidate.notification_key,
            )
        )

        return PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidateBatch(
            candidates=tuple(candidates),
            limit=limit,
        )

FILE: app\application\operations_center\platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_claims.py

"""Contracts for controlled PI escalation retry candidate claiming."""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime
from enum import StrEnum


class RetryCandidateClaimStatus(StrEnum):
    CLAIMED = "CLAIMED"
    NOT_CLAIMED = "NOT_CLAIMED"


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryClaim:
    notification_key: str
    claim_token: str
    claimed_at: datetime
    lease_expires_at: datetime
    status: RetryCandidateClaimStatus = RetryCandidateClaimStatus.CLAIMED

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        for field_name in (
            "notification_key",
            "claim_token",
        ):
            value = getattr(self, field_name)
            if not isinstance(value, str) or not value.strip():
                raise ValueError(f"{field_name} must be nonblank.")

        if self.claimed_at.tzinfo is None or self.claimed_at.utcoffset() is None:
            raise ValueError("claimed_at must be timezone-aware.")

        if (
            self.lease_expires_at.tzinfo is None
            or self.lease_expires_at.utcoffset() is None
        ):
            raise ValueError("lease_expires_at must be timezone-aware.")

        if self.lease_expires_at <= self.claimed_at:
            raise ValueError("lease_expires_at must be after claimed_at.")


============================================================
FILE: app\application\operations_center\platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_claim_service.py
============================================================
"""Application service for controlled retry candidate claiming."""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from typing import Protocol
from uuid import uuid4

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_claims import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryClaim,
    RetryCandidateClaimStatus,
)


class PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryClaimRepository(Protocol):
    async def claim_retry_candidate(
        self,
        *,
        notification_key: str,
        claim_token: str,
        claimed_at: datetime,
        lease_expires_at: datetime,
    ) -> bool: ...


class PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryClaimService:
    """Claims a candidate for one bounded execution attempt.

    Claiming is coordination only. It does not evaluate retry policy, send
    email, mutate the delivery lifecycle beyond the lease, or authorize the
    escalation itself.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        repository: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryClaimRepository,
        *,
        lease_seconds: int = 300,
    ) -> None:
        if lease_seconds < 1:
            raise ValueError("lease_seconds must be at least 1.")
        self._repository = repository
        self._lease_seconds = lease_seconds

    async def claim(
        self,
        *,
        notification_key: str,
        claimed_at: datetime | None = None,
    ) -> PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryClaim | None:
        if not isinstance(notification_key, str) or not notification_key.strip():
            raise ValueError("notification_key must be nonblank.")

        reference_at = claimed_at or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        if reference_at.tzinfo is None or reference_at.utcoffset() is None:
            raise ValueError("claimed_at must be timezone-aware.")

        claim_token = str(uuid4())
        lease_expires_at = reference_at + timedelta(seconds=self._lease_seconds)

        claimed = await self._repository.claim_retry_candidate(
            notification_key=notification_key,
            claim_token=claim_token,
            claimed_at=reference_at,
            lease_expires_at=lease_expires_at,
        )

        if not claimed:
            return None

        return PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryClaim(
            notification_key=notification_key,
            claim_token=claim_token,
            claimed_at=reference_at,
            lease_expires_at=lease_expires_at,
            status=RetryCandidateClaimStatus.CLAIMED,
        )


============================================================
FILE: app\application\operations_center\platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_execution.py
============================================================
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime
from enum import StrEnum

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_dispatch import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchRequest,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchResult,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchService,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService,
)


class PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionStatus(StrEnum):
    NOT_ELIGIBLE = "NOT_ELIGIBLE"
    NOT_DUE = "NOT_DUE"
    RETRIED = "RETRIED"


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionResult:
    status: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionStatus
    notification_key: str
    attempt: int
    reason: str
    dispatch_result: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchResult | None = None


class PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionService:
    """Execute one policy-authorized retry without owning scheduling.

    This service:
    - retrieves the authoritative durable delivery record;
    - evaluates retry policy;
    - enforces the policy-derived due time;
    - increments FAILED attempts only through the repository;
    - preserves the attempt number for STARTED recovery;
    - delegates actual delivery to the existing dispatch service.

    It does not:
    - schedule future work;
    - sleep;
    - call an email provider directly;
    - mutate delivery state directly.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        delivery_repository: object,
        retry_policy: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService,
        dispatch_service: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchService,
    ) -> None:
        if delivery_repository is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "delivery_repository cannot be None."
            )

        if retry_policy is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "retry_policy cannot be None."
            )

        if dispatch_service is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "dispatch_service cannot be None."
            )

        self._repository = delivery_repository
        self._retry_policy = retry_policy
        self._dispatch_service = dispatch_service

    async def retry(
        self,
        *,
        request: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchRequest,
        email_request: object,
        reference_at: datetime,
    ) -> PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionResult:
        record = await self._repository.get_by_notification_key(
            request.notification_key
        )

        if record is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Retry requires an existing durable delivery record."
            )

        if record.notification_key != request.notification_key:
            raise ValueError(
                "Delivery record notification key does not match request."
            )

        policy_result = self._retry_policy.evaluate(
            record=record,
            reference_at=reference_at,
        )

        if (
            policy_result.decision
            is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision.NOT_ELIGIBLE
        ):
            return PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionResult(
                status=(
                    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionStatus
                    .NOT_ELIGIBLE
                ),
                notification_key=record.notification_key,
                attempt=record.attempt,
                reason=policy_result.reason,
            )

        if (
            policy_result.next_eligible_at is not None
            and reference_at < policy_result.next_eligible_at
        ):
            return PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionResult(
                status=(
                    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionStatus
                    .NOT_DUE
                ),
                notification_key=record.notification_key,
                attempt=record.attempt,
                reason=(
                    "Retry is eligible but the policy-derived "
                    "backoff window has not elapsed."
                ),
            )

        if (
            record.status
            is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.FAILED
        ):
            await self._repository.start_retry(
                notification_key=record.notification_key,
            )

        # STARTED recovery deliberately does not call start_retry().
        # The durable attempt remains the same because this is recovery
        # of the interrupted attempt rather than a new FAILED retry.
        dispatch_result = await self._dispatch_service.dispatch(
            request=request,
            email_request=email_request,
        )

        return PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionResult(
            status=(
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionStatus
                .RETRIED
            ),
            notification_key=record.notification_key,
            attempt=(
                dispatch_result.attempt
                if dispatch_result.attempt is not None
                else record.attempt
            ),
            reason=policy_result.reason,
            dispatch_result=dispatch_result,
        )

FILE: app\application\operations_center\platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_orchestration.py
============================================================
"""Contracts for controlled PI escalation retry orchestration."""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from enum import StrEnum
from typing import Any

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_dispatch import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchRequest,
)


class PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunStatus(StrEnum):
    SUCCEEDED = "SUCCEEDED"
    SKIPPED = "SKIPPED"
    FAILED = "FAILED"


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class PlatformIntelligenceRetryState:
    """Authoritative state required immediately before retry dispatch."""

    notification_key: str
    recommendation_id: str
    recommendation_status: str
    review_status: str
    decision_status: str
    quality_gate_status: str
    dispatch_request: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchRequest
    email_request: Any

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        for field_name in (
            "notification_key",
            "recommendation_id",
            "recommendation_status",
            "review_status",
            "decision_status",
            "quality_gate_status",
        ):
            value = getattr(self, field_name)
            if not isinstance(value, str) or not value.strip():
                raise ValueError(f"{field_name} must be nonblank.")

        if self.dispatch_request.notification_key != self.notification_key:
            raise ValueError(
                "dispatch_request.notification_key must match notification_key."
            )
        if self.dispatch_request.recommendation_id != self.recommendation_id:
            raise ValueError(
                "dispatch_request.recommendation_id must match recommendation_id."
            )


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunResult:
    notification_key: str
    status: PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunStatus
    reason: str
    attempt: int | None = None
    provider_message_id: str | None = None

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not self.notification_key.strip():
            raise ValueError("notification_key must be nonblank.")
        if not self.reason.strip():
            raise ValueError("reason must be nonblank.")
        if self.attempt is not None and self.attempt < 1:
            raise ValueError("attempt must be at least 1.")

FILE: app\application\operations_center\platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_orchestrator.py
============================================================
"""Controlled orchestration for one PI escalation retry candidate."""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime
from typing import Protocol

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_claim_service import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryClaimService,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_candidates import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidate,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_execution import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionResult,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionService,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionStatus,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_orchestration import (
    PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunResult,
    PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunStatus,
    PlatformIntelligenceRetryState,
)


class PlatformIntelligenceRetryStateReader(Protocol):
    async def get_retry_state(
        self,
        *,
        notification_key: str,
        recommendation_id: str,
    ) -> PlatformIntelligenceRetryState | None: ...


class PlatformIntelligenceRetryPolicyEvaluator(Protocol):
    async def evaluate(
        self,
        *,
        candidate: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidate,
        reference_at: datetime,
    ) -> bool: ...


class PlatformIntelligenceRetryExecutor(Protocol):
    async def retry(
        self,
        *,
        request: object,
        email_request: object,
        reference_at: datetime,
    ) -> PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionResult: ...


class PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryOrchestrator:
    """Coordinates policy, durable claim, state revalidation and retry.

    This service deliberately does not discover candidates, implement retry
    policy, send email directly, or mutate human-review/risk state.

    The canonical retry execution service remains the owner of retry-policy
    enforcement and existing dispatch integration. The orchestrator only
    supplies the authoritative dispatch context after the durable claim and
    final state revalidation.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        state_reader: PlatformIntelligenceRetryStateReader,
        policy_evaluator: PlatformIntelligenceRetryPolicyEvaluator,
        claim_service: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryClaimService,
        retry_execution: PlatformIntelligenceRetryExecutor,
    ) -> None:
        self._state_reader = state_reader
        self._policy_evaluator = policy_evaluator
        self._claim_service = claim_service
        self._retry_execution = retry_execution

    async def run_candidate(
        self,
        *,
        candidate: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidate,
        reference_at: datetime,
    ) -> PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunResult:
        if reference_at.tzinfo is None or reference_at.utcoffset() is None:
            raise ValueError("reference_at must be timezone-aware.")

        eligible = await self._policy_evaluator.evaluate(
            candidate=candidate,
            reference_at=reference_at,
        )
        if not eligible:
            return PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunResult(
                notification_key=candidate.notification_key,
                status=PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunStatus.SKIPPED,
                reason="RETRY_POLICY_NOT_ELIGIBLE",
                attempt=candidate.attempt,
            )

        claim = await self._claim_service.claim(
            notification_key=candidate.notification_key,
            claimed_at=reference_at,
        )
        if claim is None:
            return PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunResult(
                notification_key=candidate.notification_key,
                status=PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunStatus.SKIPPED,
                reason="RETRY_CANDIDATE_ALREADY_CLAIMED",
                attempt=candidate.attempt,
            )

        state = await self._state_reader.get_retry_state(
            notification_key=candidate.notification_key,
            recommendation_id=candidate.recommendation_id,
        )
        if state is None:
            return self._skipped(candidate, "AUTHORITATIVE_STATE_NOT_FOUND")
        if state.notification_key != candidate.notification_key:
            return self._skipped(candidate, "NOTIFICATION_KEY_MISMATCH")
        if state.recommendation_id != candidate.recommendation_id:
            return self._skipped(candidate, "RECOMMENDATION_ID_MISMATCH")
        if state.recommendation_status != "RECOMMENDED":
            return self._skipped(
                candidate, "RECOMMENDATION_NO_LONGER_RECOMMENDED"
            )
        if state.review_status != "IN_REVIEW":
            return self._skipped(candidate, "REVIEW_NOT_IN_REVIEW")
        if state.decision_status != "PENDING_HUMAN_REVIEW":
            return self._skipped(
                candidate, "DECISION_NO_LONGER_PENDING_HUMAN_REVIEW"
            )
        if state.quality_gate_status != "PASSED":
            return self._skipped(
                candidate, "QUALITY_GATE_NO_LONGER_PASSED"
            )

        execution = await self._retry_execution.retry(
            request=state.dispatch_request,
            email_request=state.email_request,
            reference_at=reference_at,
        )

        if (
            execution.status
            is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionStatus.RETRIED
        ):
            dispatch_result = execution.dispatch_result
            return PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunResult(
                notification_key=candidate.notification_key,
                status=PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunStatus.SUCCEEDED,
                reason=execution.reason,
                attempt=execution.attempt,
                provider_message_id=(
                    dispatch_result.provider_message_id
                    if dispatch_result is not None
                    else None
                ),
            )

        if execution.status in (
            PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionStatus.NOT_ELIGIBLE,
            PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionStatus.NOT_DUE,
        ):
            return PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunResult(
                notification_key=candidate.notification_key,
                status=PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunStatus.SKIPPED,
                reason=execution.reason,
                attempt=execution.attempt,
            )

        return PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunResult(
            notification_key=candidate.notification_key,
            status=PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunStatus.FAILED,
            reason=execution.reason,
            attempt=execution.attempt,
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _skipped(
        candidate: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidate,
        reason: str,
    ) -> PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunResult:
        return PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunResult(
            notification_key=candidate.notification_key,
            status=PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunStatus.SKIPPED,
            reason=reason,
            attempt=candidate.attempt,
        )

FILE: app\application\privacy\privacy_governance_reminder_scheduler.py
============================================================
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Any, Protocol

from .privacy_governance_reminder_history import (
    PrivacyGovernanceReminderHistory,
)
from .privacy_governance_reminder_policy import (
    GovernanceReminderPolicy,
    build_decision_proposal,
)
from .privacy_governance_reminder_result import (
    PrivacyGovernanceReminderResult,
)


class GovernanceReminderStatePort(Protocol):
    def get_last_sent(self, case_id: str) -> Any:
        ...

    def record(
        self,
        *,
        case_id: str,
        sent_at: datetime,
        recipient_role: str,
        email_reference: str | None = None,
    ) -> Any:
        ...


class GovernanceReminderEmailPort(Protocol):
    def send_reminder(
        self,
        *,
        case: Any,
        proposal: Any,
    ) -> None:
        ...


class PrivacyGovernanceReminderEmailPort(Protocol):
    async def send_governance_reminder(
        self,
        *,
        case: Any,
        recipient_role: str,
        reason: str,
    ) -> Any:
        ...


class InMemoryGovernanceReminderState(
    PrivacyGovernanceReminderHistory
):
    """Process-local Beta reminder state."""

    pass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class GovernanceReminderRunResult:
    """Aggregate result for the operational follow-up invocation."""

    scanned: int
    reminded: int
    case_ids_reminded: tuple[str, ...]


class PrivacyGovernanceCaseRegistryPort(Protocol):
    def all(self) -> tuple[Any, ...]:
        ...

    def get(self, case_id: str) -> Any:
        ...


class PrivacyGovernanceReminderScheduler:
    """Single governance reminder workflow with compatibility contracts.

    Supported application contract:

        run_once()
        reminder_state
        email_sender

    Supported cybersecurity contract:

        run()
        run_case()
        history
        email_service

    This class is not an APScheduler implementation. It is an application
    workflow invoked by the approved scheduler runtime.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        case_registry: Any,
        policy: GovernanceReminderPolicy,
        reminder_state: GovernanceReminderStatePort | None = None,
        email_sender: GovernanceReminderEmailPort | None = None,
        history: PrivacyGovernanceReminderHistory | None = None,
        email_service: PrivacyGovernanceReminderEmailPort | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        if case_registry is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "case_registry cannot be None."
            )

        if policy is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "policy cannot be None."
            )

        self.case_registry = case_registry
        self.policy = policy

        self.reminder_state = (
            reminder_state
            or history
            or InMemoryGovernanceReminderState()
        )

        self.email_sender = email_sender
        self.email_service = email_service

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 6J operational follow-up API
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def run_once(
        self,
        *,
        now: datetime | None = None,
    ) -> GovernanceReminderRunResult:
        """Run the synchronous operational follow-up contract."""

        current = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)

        self._validate_now(current)

        cases = tuple(
            self.case_registry.all()
        )

        reminded: list[str] = []

        for case in cases:
            if self._run_sync_case(
                case,
                now=current,
            ):
                reminded.append(
                    case.case_id
                )

        return GovernanceReminderRunResult(
            scanned=len(cases),
            reminded=len(reminded),
            case_ids_reminded=tuple(reminded),
        )

    def _run_sync_case(
        self,
        case: Any,
        *,
        now: datetime,
    ) -> bool:
        if not self.policy.is_due(
            case,
            history=self.reminder_state,
            now=now,
        ):
            return False

        if self.email_sender is None:
            return False

        proposal = build_decision_proposal(case)

        self.email_sender.send_reminder(
            case=case,
            proposal=proposal,
        )

        self.reminder_state.record(
            case_id=case.case_id,
            sent_at=now,
            recipient_role=self.policy.recipient_role,
        )

        return True

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Existing cybersecurity async API
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    async def run(
        self,
        *,
        now: datetime | None = None,
    ) -> tuple[PrivacyGovernanceReminderResult, ...]:
        """Evaluate all cases through the async email-service contract."""

        current = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)

        self._validate_now(current)

        results: list[
            PrivacyGovernanceReminderResult
        ] = []

        for case in self.case_registry.all():
            results.append(
                await self._evaluate_case(
                    case,
                    now=current,
                )
            )

        return tuple(results)

    async def run_case(
        self,
        case_id: str,
        *,
        now: datetime | None = None,
    ) -> PrivacyGovernanceReminderResult:
        """Evaluate one case through the async contract."""

        current = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)

        self._validate_now(current)

        case = self.case_registry.get(
            case_id
        )

        return await self._evaluate_case(
            case,
            now=current,
        )

    async def _evaluate_case(
        self,
        case: Any,
        *,
        now: datetime,
    ) -> PrivacyGovernanceReminderResult:
        if not self.policy.is_eligible(case):
            return PrivacyGovernanceReminderResult(
                status="NOT_ELIGIBLE",
                case_id=case.case_id,
                governance_status=case.governance_status,
                due=False,
                recipient_role=self.policy.recipient_role,
                reason="case_not_pending_dpo_grc",
                generated_at=now,
            )

        if not self.policy.is_due(
            case,
            history=self.reminder_state,
            now=now,
        ):
            return PrivacyGovernanceReminderResult(
                status="NOT_DUE",
                case_id=case.case_id,
                governance_status=case.governance_status,
                due=False,
                recipient_role=self.policy.recipient_role,
                reason="reminder_interval_or_cooldown_not_reached",
                generated_at=now,
            )

        reason = (
            "pending_dpo_grc_reminder_due"
        )

        if self.email_service is None:
            return PrivacyGovernanceReminderResult(
                status="DUE",
                case_id=case.case_id,
                governance_status=case.governance_status,
                due=True,
                recipient_role=self.policy.recipient_role,
                reason=reason,
                generated_at=now,
                email_status="not_configured",
            )

        try:
            response = (
                await self.email_service.send_governance_reminder(
                    case=case,
                    recipient_role=self.policy.recipient_role,
                    reason=reason,
                )
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            return PrivacyGovernanceReminderResult(
                status="EMAIL_FAILED",
                case_id=case.case_id,
                governance_status=case.governance_status,
                due=True,
                recipient_role=self.policy.recipient_role,
                reason=reason,
                generated_at=now,
                email_status="failed",
                error=str(exc),
            )

        email_status = "sent"
        reference: str | None = None
        case_url: str | None = None

        if isinstance(response, dict):
            email_status = str(
                response.get(
                    "status",
                    "sent",
                )
            )

            value = (
                response.get("reference")
                or response.get("message_id")
            )

            if value is not None:
                reference = str(value)

            url = response.get("case_url")

            if url is not None:
                case_url = str(url)

        elif response is not None:
            reference = str(response)

        self.reminder_state.record(
            case_id=case.case_id,
            sent_at=now,
            recipient_role=self.policy.recipient_role,
            email_reference=reference,
        )

        return PrivacyGovernanceReminderResult(
            status="REMINDER_SENT",
            case_id=case.case_id,
            governance_status=case.governance_status,
            due=True,
            recipient_role=self.policy.recipient_role,
            reason=reason,
            generated_at=now,
            email_status=email_status,
            email_reference=reference,
            case_url=case_url,
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _validate_now(
        now: datetime,
    ) -> None:
        if now.tzinfo is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "now must be timezone-aware."
            )


__all__ = [
    "GovernanceReminderEmailPort",
    "GovernanceReminderRunResult",
    "GovernanceReminderStatePort",
    "InMemoryGovernanceReminderState",
    "PrivacyGovernanceCaseRegistryPort",
    "PrivacyGovernanceReminderEmailPort",
    "PrivacyGovernanceReminderScheduler",
]

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from uuid import UUID

from app.application.announcements.execution.contracts import (
    PublicationExecutionRequest,
    PublicationExecutionResult,
)
from app.application.announcements.execution.durable_execution_service import (
    DurablePublicationExecutionService,
)
from app.domain.announcements.publication import Publication


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class ApprovedPublicationExecutionService:
    """Application boundary from WS-23 approved state into WS-22."""

    durable_execution: DurablePublicationExecutionService

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if self.durable_execution is None:
            raise ValueError("durable_execution cannot be None.")

    async def execute(
        self,
        *,
        publication: Publication,
        actor: str,
        idempotency_key: str,
        correlation_id: str,
    ) -> PublicationExecutionResult:
        """Delegate an approved publication to the existing WS-22 service.

        WS-22 remains responsible for:
        - durable execution;
        - idempotency;
        - execution persistence;
        - execution audit;
        - retry semantics;
        - controlled provider invocation.
        """

        if publication is None:
            raise ValueError("publication cannot be None.")

        if not actor.strip():
            raise ValueError("actor cannot be empty.")

        if not idempotency_key.strip():
            raise ValueError("idempotency_key cannot be empty.")

        if not correlation_id.strip():
            raise ValueError("correlation_id cannot be empty.")

        try:
            publication_uuid = UUID(publication.publication_id)
        except (ValueError, AttributeError) as exc:
            raise ValueError(
                "Approved publication ID must be a UUID for WS-22 durable execution."
            ) from exc

        request = PublicationExecutionRequest(
            publication_id=publication_uuid,
            idempotency_key=idempotency_key.strip(),
            correlation_id=correlation_id.strip(),
            actor=actor.strip(),
        )

        return await self.durable_execution.execute(
            publication=_PublicationExecutionAdapter(publication),
            request=request,
        )


class _PublicationExecutionAdapter:
    """Adapt the WS-23 publication to the existing WS-22 execution contract."""

    def __init__(self, publication: Publication) -> None:
        self._publication = publication

    @property
    def id(self) -> UUID:
        return UUID(self._publication.publication_id)

    @property
    def title(self) -> str:
        return self._publication.title

    @property
    def content(self) -> str:
        return self._publication.content

    @property
    def status(self):
        return self._publication.status

    def begin_publish(self) -> None:
        self._publication.begin_publish()

    def mark_failure(self, reason: str) -> None:
        self._publication.mark_failure(reason)

    def publish_success(self, provider_message_id: str | None = None) -> None:
        del provider_message_id
        self._publication.publish_success()


__all__ = ["ApprovedPublicationExecutionService"]

"""WS-23.1.4 composition boundary.

Reuses the already-composed WS-22 durable execution service.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass

from app.application.announcements.execution.approved_publication_execution import (
    ApprovedPublicationExecutionService,
)
from app.application.announcements.execution.durable_execution_service import (
    DurablePublicationExecutionService,
)


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class ApprovedPublicationExecutionComposition:
    service: ApprovedPublicationExecutionService
    durable_execution: DurablePublicationExecutionService


def create_approved_publication_execution_composition(
    *,
    durable_execution: DurablePublicationExecutionService,
) -> ApprovedPublicationExecutionComposition:
    """Compose WS-23.1.4 without creating any new infrastructure."""

    if durable_execution is None:
        raise ValueError("durable_execution cannot be None.")

    service = ApprovedPublicationExecutionService(
        durable_execution=durable_execution,
    )

    return ApprovedPublicationExecutionComposition(
        service=service,
        durable_execution=durable_execution,
    )

============================================================
FILE: app\application\announcements\execution\durable_execution_composition.py
============================================================
"""WS-22.3.5.3 canonical durable publication execution composition.

This module is the composition boundary for the WS-22 durable execution
vertical slice.

It deliberately does not create a new persistence architecture. The
canonical PostgreSQL engine and async session factory are supplied by the
platform persistence boundary in:

    app.infrastructure.persistence.database

The resulting object graph is:

    PostgreSQL
        |
        v
    canonical async session factory
        |
        +--> execution repository
        |
        +--> audit repository
                  |
                  v
        DurablePublicationExecutionAudit
                  |
                  v
        DurablePublicationExecutionService

Provider selection remains outside this module.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass

from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncEngine, AsyncSession, async_sessionmaker

from app.application.announcements.execution.audit_adapter import (
    DurablePublicationExecutionAudit,
)
from app.application.announcements.execution.durable_execution_service import (
    DurablePublicationExecutionService,
)
from app.infrastructure.announcements.publication_audit_repository import (
    SqlAlchemyPublicationAuditRepository,
)
from app.infrastructure.announcements.sqlalchemy_publication_execution_repository import (
    SqlAlchemyPublicationExecutionRepository,
)
from app.infrastructure.persistence.database import (
    create_async_database_engine,
    create_async_session_factory,
)


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class DurablePublicationExecutionComposition:
    """Fully composed WS-22 durable execution boundary."""

    engine: AsyncEngine
    session_factory: async_sessionmaker[AsyncSession]
    execution_repository: SqlAlchemyPublicationExecutionRepository
    audit_repository: SqlAlchemyPublicationAuditRepository
    audit: DurablePublicationExecutionAudit
    service: DurablePublicationExecutionService

    async def dispose(self) -> None:
        """Release the shared PostgreSQL async engine."""

        await self.engine.dispose()


def create_durable_publication_execution_composition(
    *,
    publisher,
) -> DurablePublicationExecutionComposition:
    """Create the canonical WS-22 durable execution graph.

    The publisher is deliberately injected because provider selection belongs
    outside the durable execution composition.

    This function creates exactly one PostgreSQL engine and exactly one async
    session factory. Both durable repositories receive that same factory.
    """

    if publisher is None:
        raise ValueError("publisher cannot be None.")

    engine = create_async_database_engine()
    session_factory = create_async_session_factory(engine)

    execution_repository = SqlAlchemyPublicationExecutionRepository(
        session_factory
    )

    audit_repository = SqlAlchemyPublicationAuditRepository(
        session_factory
    )

    audit = DurablePublicationExecutionAudit(
        repository=audit_repository,
    )

    service = DurablePublicationExecutionService(
        publisher=publisher,
        execution_repository=execution_repository,
        audit=audit,
    )

    return DurablePublicationExecutionComposition(
        engine=engine,
        session_factory=session_factory,
        execution_repository=execution_repository,
        audit_repository=audit_repository,
        audit=audit,
        service=service,
    )


__all__ = [
    "DurablePublicationExecutionComposition",
    "create_durable_publication_execution_composition",
]


============================================================
FILE: app\application\announcements\execution\durable_execution_service.py
============================================================
"""WS-22.3.2 durable execution orchestration."""

from __future__ import annotations

from app.application.announcements.execution.contracts import (
    AnnouncementPublisher,
    PublicationExecutionRequest,
    PublicationExecutionResult,
    PublicationExecutionStatus,
)
from app.application.announcements.execution.durable_contracts import (
    DurableExecutionStatus,
    PublicationExecutionRepository,
)


class DurablePublicationExecutionService:
    """Retry-safe execution service backed by durable idempotency state.

    A completed idempotency key is never sent again.

    A STARTED record is treated as an interrupted attempt and is retryable.

    A FAILED record is also retryable. Before a retry is executed, the
    repository is responsible for incrementing the durable attempt counter
    and transitioning the record back to STARTED.

    The repository implementation must make creation atomic against a
    unique idempotency key.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        publisher: AnnouncementPublisher,
        execution_repository: PublicationExecutionRepository,
        audit,
    ) -> None:
        if publisher is None:
            raise ValueError("publisher cannot be None.")

        if execution_repository is None:
            raise ValueError("execution_repository cannot be None.")

        if audit is None:
            raise ValueError("audit cannot be None.")

        self._publisher = publisher
        self._repository = execution_repository
        self._audit = audit

    async def execute(
        self,
        *,
        publication,
        request: PublicationExecutionRequest,
    ) -> PublicationExecutionResult:
        existing = await self._repository.get_by_idempotency_key(
            request.idempotency_key
        )

        # A successful execution is terminal for the idempotency key.
        # Never call the external provider again.
        if (
            existing is not None
            and existing.status is DurableExecutionStatus.SUCCEEDED
        ):
            return PublicationExecutionResult(
                publication_id=existing.publication_id,
                status=PublicationExecutionStatus.IDEMPOTENT_REPLAY,
                idempotency_key=existing.idempotency_key,
                correlation_id=request.correlation_id,
                provider_message_id=existing.provider_message_id,
            )

        self._assert_approved(publication)

        # The repository owns the atomic unique-key operation. If another
        # worker already created the same key, re-read and honour its state.
        if existing is None:
            try:
                record = await self._repository.create_started(
                    publication_id=publication.id,
                    idempotency_key=request.idempotency_key,
                    correlation_id=request.correlation_id,
                )
            except Exception:
                record = await self._repository.get_by_idempotency_key(
                    request.idempotency_key
                )

                if record is None:
                    raise

        elif existing.status is DurableExecutionStatus.FAILED:
            # A FAILED execution is retryable. Reuse the same durable
            # idempotency record and increment its attempt number rather
            # than creating a second row.
            record = await self._repository.start_retry(
                idempotency_key=request.idempotency_key,
            )

        else:
            # STARTED represents an interrupted/incomplete attempt.
            # Preserve the existing durable attempt number and retry it.
            record = existing

        publication.begin_publish()

        try:
            provider_message_id = await self._publisher.publish(
                publication_id=publication.id,
                title=publication.title,
                content=publication.content,
                correlation_id=request.correlation_id,
                idempotency_key=request.idempotency_key,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            error_code = self._error_code(exc)

            publication.mark_failure(error_code)

            await self._repository.mark_failed(
                idempotency_key=request.idempotency_key,
                error_code=error_code,
            )

            await self._audit.record_failure(
                publication_id=publication.id,
                actor=request.actor,
                correlation_id=request.correlation_id,
                error_code=error_code,
            )

            return PublicationExecutionResult(
                publication_id=publication.id,
                status=PublicationExecutionStatus.FAILED,
                idempotency_key=request.idempotency_key,
                correlation_id=request.correlation_id,
                error_code=error_code,
            )

        publication.publish_success(provider_message_id)

        await self._repository.mark_succeeded(
            idempotency_key=request.idempotency_key,
            provider_message_id=provider_message_id,
        )

        await self._audit.record_success(
            publication_id=publication.id,
            actor=request.actor,
            correlation_id=request.correlation_id,
            provider_message_id=provider_message_id,
        )

        return PublicationExecutionResult(
            publication_id=publication.id,
            status=PublicationExecutionStatus.SUCCEEDED,
            idempotency_key=request.idempotency_key,
            correlation_id=request.correlation_id,
            provider_message_id=provider_message_id,
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _assert_approved(publication) -> None:
        value = getattr(publication.status, "value", publication.status)

        if value != "approved":
            raise ValueError(
                "Only approved publications may be executed."
            )

    @staticmethod
    def _error_code(exc: Exception) -> str:
        explicit = getattr(exc, "error_code", None)

        return (
            explicit.strip()[:100]
            if isinstance(explicit, str) and explicit.strip()
            else type(exc).__name__.upper()[:100]
        )


============================================================
FILE: app\application\announcements\execution\publication_execution_service.py
============================================================
"""WS-22.3.1 provider-independent publication execution service."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Protocol
from uuid import UUID

from app.application.announcements.execution.contracts import (
    AnnouncementPublisher,
    PublicationExecutionRequest,
    PublicationExecutionResult,
    PublicationExecutionStatus,
)


class ExecutablePublication(Protocol):
    """Minimum aggregate surface required by the execution service."""

    id: UUID
    title: str
    content: str
    status: object

    def begin_publish(self) -> None: ...

    def publish_success(self, provider_message_id: str | None = None) -> None: ...

    def mark_failure(self, reason: str) -> None: ...


class PublicationExecutionAudit(Protocol):
    """Minimal audit boundary used by execution.

    The concrete publication-event repository remains an existing
    application concern; this protocol prevents execution from coupling to
    a particular persistence implementation.
    """

    async def record_success(
        self,
        *,
        publication_id: UUID,
        actor: str,
        correlation_id: str,
        provider_message_id: str | None,
    ) -> None: ...

    async def record_failure(
        self,
        *,
        publication_id: UUID,
        actor: str,
        correlation_id: str,
        error_code: str,
    ) -> None: ...


class PublicationExecutionService:
    """Execute approved publications through a provider-independent port.

    The service owns orchestration and idempotency. It does not know which
    channel/provider performs the external publication.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        publisher: AnnouncementPublisher,
        audit: PublicationExecutionAudit,
    ) -> None:
        if publisher is None:
            raise ValueError("publisher cannot be None.")
        if audit is None:
            raise ValueError("audit cannot be None.")
        self._publisher = publisher
        self._audit = audit
        self._completed: dict[str, PublicationExecutionResult] = {}

    async def execute(
        self,
        *,
        publication: ExecutablePublication,
        request: PublicationExecutionRequest,
    ) -> PublicationExecutionResult:
        if publication is None:
            raise ValueError("publication cannot be None.")

        existing = self._completed.get(request.idempotency_key)
        if existing is not None:
            return PublicationExecutionResult(
                publication_id=existing.publication_id,
                status=PublicationExecutionStatus.IDEMPOTENT_REPLAY,
                idempotency_key=existing.idempotency_key,
                correlation_id=request.correlation_id,
                provider_message_id=existing.provider_message_id,
                error_code=existing.error_code,
                error_message=existing.error_message,
            )

        self._assert_approved(publication)
        publication.begin_publish()

        try:
            provider_message_id = await self._publisher.publish(
                publication_id=publication.id,
                title=publication.title,
                content=publication.content,
                correlation_id=request.correlation_id,
                idempotency_key=request.idempotency_key,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            error_code = self._error_code(exc)
            publication.mark_failure(error_code)
            await self._audit.record_failure(
                publication_id=publication.id,
                actor=request.actor,
                correlation_id=request.correlation_id,
                error_code=error_code,
            )
            result = PublicationExecutionResult(
                publication_id=publication.id,
                status=PublicationExecutionStatus.FAILED,
                idempotency_key=request.idempotency_key,
                correlation_id=request.correlation_id,
                error_code=error_code,
            )
            return result

        publication.publish_success(provider_message_id)
        await self._audit.record_success(
            publication_id=publication.id,
            actor=request.actor,
            correlation_id=request.correlation_id,
            provider_message_id=provider_message_id,
        )

        result = PublicationExecutionResult(
            publication_id=publication.id,
            status=PublicationExecutionStatus.SUCCEEDED,
            idempotency_key=request.idempotency_key,
            correlation_id=request.correlation_id,
            provider_message_id=provider_message_id,
        )
        self._completed[request.idempotency_key] = result
        return result

    @staticmethod
    def _assert_approved(publication: ExecutablePublication) -> None:
        status_value = getattr(publication.status, "value", publication.status)
        if status_value != "approved":
            raise ValueError(
                "Only approved publications may be executed."
            )

    @staticmethod
    def _error_code(exc: Exception) -> str:
        """Return a stable, non-sensitive operational error code."""
        explicit = getattr(exc, "error_code", None)
        if isinstance(explicit, str) and explicit.strip():
            return explicit.strip()[:100]
        return type(exc).__name__.upper()[:100]
