FILE: app\application\announcement_operations\publication_execution_ports.py
============================================================
"""WS-22.3.1 provider/channel-independent ports."""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Protocol

from app.domain.announcement_publication.execution import (
    PublishAnnouncementCommand,
    PublicationExecutionResult,
)


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class PublicationForExecution:
    """Minimal execution projection required by the use case.

    The adapter/application layer can map the existing WS-22.1
    publication model into this projection without coupling the execution
    engine to a specific channel provider.
    """

    publication_id: str
    status: str
    destination: str
    rendered_content: str


class PublicationExecutionRepository(Protocol):
    """Port for loading and recording publication execution state."""

    async def get_for_execution(
        self,
        publication_id: str,
    ) -> PublicationForExecution | None:
        ...

    async def record_execution(
        self,
        result: PublicationExecutionResult,
    ) -> None:
        ...


class PublicationPublisher(Protocol):
    """Provider/channel-neutral publishing port."""

    async def publish(
        self,
        *,
        publication: PublicationForExecution,
        idempotency_key: str,
    ) -> str:
        """Publish and return an opaque provider reference.

        Implementations must not expose credentials through the result.
        """
        ...


class PublicationAuditSink(Protocol):
    """Port for durable/observable publication audit events."""

    async def record(self, event: object) -> None:
        ...


class PublicationIdempotencyStore(Protocol):
    """Port preventing duplicate external publication attempts."""

    async def get_result(
        self,
        idempotency_key: str,
    ) -> PublicationExecutionResult | None:
        ...

    async def store_result(
        self,
        result: PublicationExecutionResult,
    ) -> None:
        ...


__all__ = [
    "PublicationAuditSink",
    "PublicationExecutionRepository",
    "PublicationExecutionResult",
    "PublicationForExecution",
    "PublicationIdempotencyStore",
    "PublicationPublisher",
]



============================================================
FILE: app\application\announcement_operations\publication_execution_service.py
============================================================
"""WS-22.3.1 publication execution application contract."""

from __future__ import annotations

from app.application.announcement_operations.publication_execution_ports import (
    PublicationAuditSink,
    PublicationExecutionRepository,
    PublicationIdempotencyStore,
    PublicationPublisher,
)
from app.domain.announcement_publication.execution import (
    PublishAnnouncementCommand,
    PublicationAuditEvent,
    PublicationExecutionErrorCode,
    PublicationExecutionResult,
)


class PublicationExecutionService:
    """Execute an approved publication through an injected publishing port.

    This service contains orchestration only. It does not know Telegram,
    HTTP, frontend concerns, credentials, or provider-specific APIs.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        repository: PublicationExecutionRepository,
        publisher: PublicationPublisher,
        idempotency_store: PublicationIdempotencyStore,
        audit_sink: PublicationAuditSink,
    ) -> None:
        if any(
            dependency is None
            for dependency in (
                repository,
                publisher,
                idempotency_store,
                audit_sink,
            )
        ):
            raise ValueError("All publication execution dependencies are required.")

        self._repository = repository
        self._publisher = publisher
        self._idempotency_store = idempotency_store
        self._audit_sink = audit_sink

    async def execute(
        self,
        command: PublishAnnouncementCommand,
    ) -> PublicationExecutionResult:
        """Execute one explicit publication request."""

        existing = await self._idempotency_store.get_result(
            command.idempotency_key
        )
        if existing is not None:
            return existing

        publication = await self._repository.get_for_execution(
            command.publication_id
        )

        if publication is None:
            result = PublicationExecutionResult.failed(
                publication_id=command.publication_id,
                idempotency_key=command.idempotency_key,
                error_code=PublicationExecutionErrorCode.INVALID_STATE,
                error_message="Publication is not available for execution.",
            )
            await self._record_result(command, result)
            return result

        if publication.status.lower() not in {"approved", "ready_to_publish"}:
            result = PublicationExecutionResult.failed(
                publication_id=command.publication_id,
                idempotency_key=command.idempotency_key,
                error_code=PublicationExecutionErrorCode.INVALID_STATE,
                error_message="Publication is not in an executable state.",
            )
            await self._record_result(command, result)
            return result

        try:
            provider_reference = await self._publisher.publish(
                publication=publication,
                idempotency_key=command.idempotency_key,
            )
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
            result = PublicationExecutionResult.failed(
                publication_id=command.publication_id,
                idempotency_key=command.idempotency_key,
                error_code=PublicationExecutionErrorCode.PROVIDER_ERROR,
                error_message=str(exc) or "Publication provider failed.",
            )
            await self._record_result(command, result)
            return result

        result = PublicationExecutionResult.succeeded(
            publication_id=command.publication_id,
            idempotency_key=command.idempotency_key,
            provider_reference=provider_reference,
        )
        await self._record_result(command, result)
        return result

    async def _record_result(
        self,
        command: PublishAnnouncementCommand,
        result: PublicationExecutionResult,
    ) -> None:
        await self._idempotency_store.store_result(result)
        await self._repository.record_execution(result)

        await self._audit_sink.record(
            PublicationAuditEvent(
                event_name="announcement_publication_execution",
                publication_id=result.publication_id,
                requested_by=command.requested_by,
                status=result.status,
                occurred_at=result.executed_at,
                idempotency_key=result.idempotency_key,
                provider_reference=result.provider_reference,
                error_code=result.error_code,
                safe_metadata={"execution_status": result.status.value},
            )
        )


__all__ = ["PublicationExecutionService"]



============================================================
FILE: app\infrastructure\announcements\sqlalchemy_publication_execution_repository.py
============================================================
"""SQLAlchemy durable execution repository for WS-22.

Uses the canonical EasyChatBot SQLAlchemy Base. This module deliberately does
not create a second DeclarativeBase/metadata registry.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timezone
from uuid import UUID

from sqlalchemy import DateTime, Integer, String, select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession, async_sessionmaker
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column

from app.application.announcements.execution.durable_contracts import (
    DurableExecutionStatus,
    PublicationExecutionRecord,
    PublicationExecutionRepository,
)
from app.infrastructure.persistence.database import Base


class PublicationExecutionRecordModel(Base):
    """Durable operational state for a WS-22 publication execution."""

    __tablename__ = "announcement_publication_executions"

    publication_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(36), nullable=False)
    idempotency_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(255), primary_key=True
    )
    correlation_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(255),
        nullable=False,
    )
    attempt: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        Integer,
        nullable=False,
        default=1,
    )
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(32),
        nullable=False,
    )
    provider_message_id: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
        String(255),
        nullable=True,
    )
    error_code: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
        String(100),
        nullable=True,
    )
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True),
        nullable=False,
    )
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True),
        nullable=False,
    )


class SqlAlchemyPublicationExecutionRepository(
    PublicationExecutionRepository
):
    """Durable repository using the existing injected async session factory."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        session_factory: async_sessionmaker[AsyncSession],
    ) -> None:
        if session_factory is None:
            raise ValueError("session_factory cannot be None.")

        self._session_factory = session_factory

    async def get_by_idempotency_key(
        self,
        idempotency_key: str,
    ) -> PublicationExecutionRecord | None:
        async with self._session_factory() as session:
            result = await session.execute(
                select(PublicationExecutionRecordModel).where(
                    PublicationExecutionRecordModel.idempotency_key
                    == idempotency_key
                )
            )
            model = result.scalar_one_or_none()

            return self._to_domain(model) if model else None

    async def create_started(
        self,
        *,
        publication_id,
        idempotency_key,
        correlation_id,
    ) -> PublicationExecutionRecord:
        """Create the first durable execution attempt."""

        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

        model = PublicationExecutionRecordModel(
            publication_id=str(publication_id),
            idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
            correlation_id=correlation_id,
            attempt=1,
            status=DurableExecutionStatus.STARTED.value,
            created_at=now,
            updated_at=now,
        )

        async with self._session_factory() as session:
            session.add(model)
            await session.commit()
            await session.refresh(model)

            return self._to_domain(model)

    async def mark_succeeded(
        self,
        *,
        idempotency_key,
        provider_message_id,
    ) -> PublicationExecutionRecord:
        """Persist successful completion without changing the attempt count."""

        return await self._update(
            idempotency_key,
            status=DurableExecutionStatus.SUCCEEDED.value,
            provider_message_id=provider_message_id,
            error_code=None,
        )

    async def mark_failed(
        self,
        *,
        idempotency_key,
        error_code,
    ) -> PublicationExecutionRecord:
        """Persist failure while retaining the current attempt number."""

        return await self._update(
            idempotency_key,
            status=DurableExecutionStatus.FAILED.value,
            error_code=error_code,
        )

    async def start_retry(
        self,
        *,
        idempotency_key: str,
    ) -> PublicationExecutionRecord:
        """Increment the durable attempt counter for a retry.

        A retry is valid only for an existing FAILED execution. The same
        idempotency record is retained; no second database row is created.
        """

        async with self._session_factory() as session:
            result = await session.execute(
                select(PublicationExecutionRecordModel).where(
                    PublicationExecutionRecordModel.idempotency_key
                    == idempotency_key
                )
            )

            model = result.scalar_one()

            if model.status != DurableExecutionStatus.FAILED.value:
                raise ValueError(
                    "Only failed executions can be retried."
                )

            model.attempt += 1
            model.status = DurableExecutionStatus.STARTED.value
            model.error_code = None
            model.provider_message_id = None
            model.updated_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

            await session.commit()
            await session.refresh(model)

            return self._to_domain(model)

    async def _update(
        self,
        idempotency_key,
        **values,
    ) -> PublicationExecutionRecord:
        async with self._session_factory() as session:
            result = await session.execute(
                select(PublicationExecutionRecordModel).where(
                    PublicationExecutionRecordModel.idempotency_key
                    == idempotency_key
                )
            )

            model = result.scalar_one()

            for key, value in values.items():
                setattr(model, key, value)

            model.updated_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

            await session.commit()
            await session.refresh(model)

            return self._to_domain(model)

    @staticmethod
    def _to_domain(
        model: PublicationExecutionRecordModel,
    ) -> PublicationExecutionRecord:
        return PublicationExecutionRecord(
            publication_id=UUID(model.publication_id),
            idempotency_key=model.idempotency_key,
            correlation_id=model.correlation_id,
            attempt=model.attempt,
            status=DurableExecutionStatus(model.status),
            provider_message_id=model.provider_message_id,
            error_code=model.error_code,
            created_at=model.created_at,
            updated_at=model.updated_at,
        )

FILE: app\api\dependencies\authorization.py

from __future__ import annotations

from fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, status

from app.api.dependencies.identity import get_current_principal
from app.application.authorization.authorization_service import (
    AuthorizationService,
    PermissionDeniedError,
)
from app.domain.authorization.permissions import Permission
from app.domain.identity.principal import Principal


def require_permission(permission: Permission):
    async def dependency(
        principal: Principal = Depends(get_current_principal),
    ) -> Principal:
        try:
            return AuthorizationService().require_permission(
                principal=principal,
                permission=permission,
            )
        except PermissionDeniedError:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
                detail="Insufficient permission.",
            ) from None

    return dependency


============================================================
FILE: app\api\dependencies\identity.py
============================================================
from __future__ import annotations

from fastapi import HTTPException, Request, status

from app.domain.identity.principal import Principal
from app.infrastructure.auth.session_identity_provider import (
    SessionIdentityProvider,
)


async def get_current_principal(request: Request) -> Principal:
    """Resolve identity from the HttpOnly application session cookie."""

    session_id = request.cookies.get("easychatbot_session")

    repository = request.app.state.auth_repository

    provider = SessionIdentityProvider(
        session_id=session_id,
        repository=repository,
    )

    principal = await provider.get_current_principal()

    if principal is None or not principal.is_active:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
            detail="Authentication required.",
        )

    return principal

from types import SimpleNamespace

from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from fastapi import FastAPI

from app.api.operations_center import create_operations_center_router
from app.application.operations_center.dashboard_composition import (
    create_operations_center_composition,
)


class Source:
    def _item(self):
        return SimpleNamespace(
            review_item_id="R1",
            risk_decision_id="D1",
            risk_assessment_id="A1",
            scope="platform",
            risk_level="HIGH",
            risk_score=0.8,
            priority="HIGH",
            review_status="PENDING",
            decision_status="PENDING_HUMAN_REVIEW",
            quality_gate_status="PASSED",
            evidence_references=("E1",),
            historical_references=("H1",),
            policy_id="P",
            policy_version="1",
            created_at=SimpleNamespace(
                isoformat=lambda: "2026-09-22T00:00:00+00:00"
            ),
            assigned_reviewer=None,
            assigned_at=None,
            review_due_at=None,
            rationale="test",
            recommendation_status="RECOMMENDED",
            recommendation_route="HUMAN_REVIEW",
            recommendation_reasons=("risk",),
            recommendation_id="REC1",
        )

    def list_reviews(self):
        return [self._item()]

    def get_review(self, review_item_id):
        return self._item() if review_item_id == "R1" else None

    def list_escalations(self):
        return [self._item()]


def _client(source=None, monkeypatch=None):
    app = FastAPI()
    if monkeypatch is not None:
        import app.api.operations_center as operations_center_module
        monkeypatch.setattr(
            operations_center_module,
            "require_permission",
            lambda _permission: (lambda: None),
        )
    composition = create_operations_center_composition(
        platform_intelligence_review_source=source
    )
    app.include_router(create_operations_center_router(composition=composition))
    return TestClient(app)


def test_existing_operations_center_dashboard_route_is_preserved(monkeypatch):
    client = _client(Source(), monkeypatch)
    response = client.get("/api/operations-center/dashboard")
    assert response.status_code == 200


def test_pi_reviews_are_exposed_through_existing_operations_center_router(monkeypatch):
    client = _client(Source(), monkeypatch)
    response = client.get("/api/operations-center/platform-intelligence/reviews")
    assert response.status_code == 200
    assert response.json()["count"] == 1


def test_pi_escalations_are_exposed_through_existing_operations_center_router(monkeypatch):
    client = _client(Source(), monkeypatch)
    response = client.get("/api/operations-center/platform-intelligence/escalations")
    assert response.status_code == 200
    assert response.json()["count"] == 1


def test_pi_review_detail_not_found_is_404(monkeypatch):
    client = _client(Source(), monkeypatch)
    response = client.get(
        "/api/operations-center/platform-intelligence/reviews/UNKNOWN"
    )
    assert response.status_code == 404


def test_pi_integration_fails_closed_when_source_is_not_configured(monkeypatch):
    client = _client(monkeypatch=monkeypatch)
    response = client.get("/api/operations-center/platform-intelligence/reviews")
    assert response.status_code == 503

from datetime import datetime, timezone
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_review_api import (
    PlatformIntelligenceReviewAPI,
    PlatformIntelligenceReviewNotConfigured,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_review_source import (
    PlatformIntelligenceReviewSource,
)


class FakeSource(PlatformIntelligenceReviewSource):
    def __init__(self, items):
        self.items = tuple(items)

    def list_reviews(self):
        return self.items

    def get_review(self, review_item_id):
        return next(
            (item for item in self.items if item.review_item_id == review_item_id),
            None,
        )

    def list_escalations(self):
        return tuple(
            item
            for item in self.items
            if getattr(
                getattr(item, "recommendation_status", None),
                "value",
                getattr(item, "recommendation_status", None),
            )
            == "RECOMMENDED"
        )


def projection(*, recommended=False):
    return SimpleNamespace(
        review_item_id="review-1",
        risk_decision_id="decision-1",
        risk_assessment_id="assessment-1",
        scope="platform",
        risk_level=SimpleNamespace(value="HIGH"),
        risk_score=0.8,
        priority=SimpleNamespace(value="HIGH"),
        review_status=SimpleNamespace(value="IN_REVIEW"),
        decision_status=SimpleNamespace(value="PENDING_HUMAN_REVIEW"),
        quality_gate_status=SimpleNamespace(value="PASSED"),
        evidence_references=("e1",),
        historical_references=("h1",),
        policy_id="risk-policy",
        policy_version="1.0",
        created_at=datetime(2026, 9, 22, tzinfo=timezone.utc),
        assigned_reviewer="reviewer-1",
        assigned_at=datetime(2026, 9, 22, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc),
        review_due_at=None,
        rationale="Governed review required.",
        recommendation_status=(
            SimpleNamespace(value="RECOMMENDED") if recommended else None
        ),
        recommendation_route=(
            SimpleNamespace(value="RISK_OWNER") if recommended else None
        ),
        recommendation_reasons=("risk_level=HIGH",) if recommended else (),
        recommendation_id="esc-1" if recommended else None,
        metadata={"sensitive": "must-not-leak"},
    )


def test_list_serializes_only_approved_projection_fields():
    api = PlatformIntelligenceReviewAPI(source=FakeSource([projection()]))

    result = api.list_reviews()

    assert result["status"] == "ready"
    assert result["count"] == 1
    assert result["items"][0]["risk_decision_id"] == "decision-1"
    assert "metadata" not in result["items"][0]


def test_get_missing_returns_none():
    api = PlatformIntelligenceReviewAPI(source=FakeSource([]))

    assert api.get_review("missing") is None


def test_escalations_only_include_recommended_items():
    api = PlatformIntelligenceReviewAPI(
        source=FakeSource([projection(), projection(recommended=True)])
    )

    result = api.list_escalations()

    assert result["count"] == 1
    assert result["items"][0]["recommendation_status"] == "RECOMMENDED"


def test_unconfigured_source_is_explicitly_not_configured():
    api = PlatformIntelligenceReviewAPI(source=None)

    with pytest.raises(PlatformIntelligenceReviewNotConfigured):
        api.list_reviews()

FILE: tests\unit\operations_center\test_platform_intelligence_retry_worker.py

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timezone

import pytest

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_candidates import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidateBatch,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_orchestration import (
    PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunResult,
    PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunStatus,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_retry_worker import (
    PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemStatus,
    PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerRequest,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_retry_worker_service import (
    PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorker,
)


REFERENCE = datetime(2026, 9, 22, 12, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)


class Candidate:
    def __init__(self, key: str, attempt: int = 1) -> None:
        self.notification_key = key
        self.recommendation_id = f"rec-{key}"
        self.attempt = attempt


class Discovery:
    def __init__(self, candidates) -> None:
        self.candidates = tuple(candidates)
        self.calls = 0
        self.limit = None

    async def discover(self, *, limit: int):
        self.calls += 1
        self.limit = limit
        return PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidateBatch(
            candidates=self.candidates,
            limit=limit,
        )


class Orchestrator:
    def __init__(self, results=None, errors=None) -> None:
        self.results = results or {}
        self.errors = errors or {}
        self.calls = []

    async def run_candidate(self, *, candidate, reference_at):
        self.calls.append((candidate.notification_key, reference_at))
        if candidate.notification_key in self.errors:
            raise self.errors[candidate.notification_key]
        return self.results[candidate.notification_key]


def run_result(key, status, reason, attempt=1, provider=None):
    return PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunResult(
        notification_key=key,
        status=status,
        reason=reason,
        attempt=attempt,
        provider_message_id=provider,
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_empty_batch_is_a_noop():
    discovery = Discovery([])
    orchestrator = Orchestrator()

    worker = PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorker(
        discovery_service=discovery,
        orchestrator=orchestrator,
    )

    result = await worker.run(
        request=PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerRequest(limit=10),
        reference_at=REFERENCE,
    )

    assert result.discovered_count == 0
    assert result.processed_count == 0
    assert result.succeeded_count == 0
    assert result.skipped_count == 0
    assert result.failed_count == 0
    assert result.items == ()
    assert discovery.calls == 1
    assert discovery.limit == 10
    assert orchestrator.calls == []


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_worker_processes_bounded_batch_and_preserves_results():
    candidates = [Candidate("key-a"), Candidate("key-b"), Candidate("key-c")]
    discovery = Discovery(candidates)
    orchestrator = Orchestrator(
        results={
            "key-a": run_result(
                "key-a",
                PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunStatus.SUCCEEDED,
                "RETRY_DISPATCHED",
                attempt=2,
                provider="provider-a",
            ),
            "key-b": run_result(
                "key-b",
                PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunStatus.SKIPPED,
                "RETRY_POLICY_NOT_ELIGIBLE",
            ),
            "key-c": run_result(
                "key-c",
                PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunStatus.FAILED,
                "DISPATCH_FAILED",
            ),
        }
    )

    worker = PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorker(
        discovery_service=discovery,
        orchestrator=orchestrator,
    )

    result = await worker.run(
        request=PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerRequest(limit=3),
        reference_at=REFERENCE,
    )

    assert result.discovered_count == 3
    assert result.processed_count == 3
    assert result.succeeded_count == 1
    assert result.skipped_count == 1
    assert result.failed_count == 1
    assert [item.notification_key for item in result.items] == [
        "key-a",
        "key-b",
        "key-c",
    ]
    assert result.items[0].status is PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemStatus.SUCCEEDED
    assert result.items[0].provider_message_id == "provider-a"
    assert result.items[1].status is PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemStatus.SKIPPED
    assert result.items[2].status is PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemStatus.FAILED
    assert orchestrator.calls == [
        ("key-a", REFERENCE),
        ("key-b", REFERENCE),
        ("key-c", REFERENCE),
    ]


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_one_candidate_exception_does_not_abort_remaining_candidates():
    candidates = [Candidate("key-a"), Candidate("key-b"), Candidate("key-c")]
    discovery = Discovery(candidates)
    orchestrator = Orchestrator(
        results={
            "key-a": run_result(
                "key-a",
                PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunStatus.SUCCEEDED,
                "RETRY_DISPATCHED",
            ),
            "key-c": run_result(
                "key-c",
                PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunStatus.SKIPPED,
                "RETRY_CANDIDATE_ALREADY_CLAIMED",
            ),
        },
        errors={"key-b": RuntimeError("provider failure")},
    )

    worker = PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorker(
        discovery_service=discovery,
        orchestrator=orchestrator,
    )

    result = await worker.run(
        request=PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerRequest(limit=3),
        reference_at=REFERENCE,
    )

    assert result.processed_count == 3
    assert result.succeeded_count == 1
    assert result.skipped_count == 1
    assert result.failed_count == 1

    failed = result.items[1]
    assert failed.notification_key == "key-b"
    assert failed.reason == "CANDIDATE_EXECUTION_ERROR"
    assert failed.error_type == "RuntimeError"

    assert len(orchestrator.calls) == 3


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_worker_rejects_naive_reference_time():
    worker = PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorker(
        discovery_service=Discovery([]),
        orchestrator=Orchestrator(),
    )

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="timezone-aware"):
        await worker.run(
            request=PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerRequest(limit=1),
            reference_at=datetime(2026, 9, 22, 12, 0),
        )


def test_worker_request_rejects_invalid_limit():
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="at least 1"):
        PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerRequest(limit=0)


def test_worker_result_rejects_duplicate_notification_keys():
    from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_retry_worker import (
        PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemResult,
        PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerResult,
    )

    item = PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemResult(
        notification_key="duplicate",
        status=PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemStatus.SKIPPED,
        reason="SKIPPED",
        attempt=1,
    )

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="duplicate"):
        PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerResult(
            requested_limit=2,
            discovered_count=2,
            processed_count=2,
            succeeded_count=0,
            skipped_count=2,
            failed_count=0,
            items=(item, item),
        )

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

import pytest

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicy,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyStatus,
)


REFERENCE = datetime(
    2026,
    9,
    22,
    10,
    0,
    tzinfo=timezone.utc,
)


@dataclass
class Record:
    notification_key: str
    recommendation_id: str
    risk_decision_id: str
    review_item_id: str
    correlation_id: str
    attempt: int
    status: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus
    provider_message_id: str | None = None
    error_code: str | None = None
    created_at: datetime | None = REFERENCE
    updated_at: datetime | None = REFERENCE


def policy(**overrides):
    values = {
        "policy_id": "PI-RETRY",
        "policy_version": "1.0",
        "status": (
            PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyStatus
            .APPROVED
        ),
        "max_attempts": 4,
        "base_backoff_seconds": 60,
        "max_backoff_seconds": 300,
        "retryable_error_codes": frozenset(
            {
                "TIMEOUT",
                "CONNECTION_ERROR",
                "PROVIDER_ERROR",
            }
        ),
        "retry_interrupted_started": True,
    }

    values.update(overrides)

    return PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicy(
        **values
    )


def record(
    *,
    status=PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.FAILED,
    attempt=1,
    error_code="PROVIDER_ERROR",
):
    return Record(
        notification_key=(
            "platform_intelligence_escalation:REC-1"
        ),
        recommendation_id="REC-1",
        risk_decision_id="DEC-1",
        review_item_id="REV-1",
        correlation_id="CORR-1",
        attempt=attempt,
        status=status,
        error_code=error_code,
        created_at=REFERENCE,
        updated_at=REFERENCE,
    )


def test_retryable_failed_delivery_is_eligible_with_deterministic_backoff():
    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService(
        policy=policy()
    )

    result = service.evaluate(
        record=record(attempt=1),
        reference_at=REFERENCE + timedelta(seconds=60),
    )

    assert (
        result.decision
        is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision.ELIGIBLE
    )

    assert result.backoff_seconds == 60

    assert result.next_eligible_at == (
        REFERENCE + timedelta(seconds=60)
    )

    assert result.policy_id == "PI-RETRY"
    assert result.policy_version == "1.0"


def test_failed_delivery_is_not_due_before_backoff():
    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService(
        policy=policy()
    )

    result = service.evaluate(
        record=record(attempt=1),
        reference_at=REFERENCE + timedelta(seconds=59),
    )

    assert (
        result.decision
        is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision.ELIGIBLE
    )

    assert result.backoff_seconds == 60

    assert result.next_eligible_at == (
        REFERENCE + timedelta(seconds=60)
    )


def test_backoff_doubles_and_is_capped():
    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService(
        policy=policy()
    )

    assert service.backoff_seconds(attempt=1) == 60
    assert service.backoff_seconds(attempt=2) == 120
    assert service.backoff_seconds(attempt=3) == 240
    assert service.backoff_seconds(attempt=4) == 300
    assert service.backoff_seconds(attempt=10) == 300


def test_non_retryable_error_is_not_eligible():
    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService(
        policy=policy()
    )

    result = service.evaluate(
        record=record(
            error_code="INVALID_RECIPIENT"
        ),
        reference_at=REFERENCE,
    )

    assert (
        result.decision
        is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision.NOT_ELIGIBLE
    )

    assert "not retryable" in result.reason


def test_missing_error_code_is_not_eligible():
    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService(
        policy=policy()
    )

    result = service.evaluate(
        record=record(error_code=None),
        reference_at=REFERENCE,
    )

    assert (
        result.decision
        is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision.NOT_ELIGIBLE
    )

    assert "no error code" in result.reason


def test_maximum_attempts_is_enforced():
    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService(
        policy=policy()
    )

    result = service.evaluate(
        record=record(attempt=4),
        reference_at=REFERENCE,
    )

    assert (
        result.decision
        is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision.NOT_ELIGIBLE
    )

    assert "Maximum delivery attempts" in result.reason


def test_draft_policy_cannot_authorize_retry():
    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService(
        policy=policy(
            status=(
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyStatus
                .DRAFT
            )
        )
    )

    result = service.evaluate(
        record=record(),
        reference_at=REFERENCE,
    )

    assert (
        result.decision
        is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision.NOT_ELIGIBLE
    )

    assert "not APPROVED" in result.reason


def test_interrupted_started_delivery_is_policy_controlled():
    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService(
        policy=policy()
    )

    result = service.evaluate(
        record=record(
            status=(
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus
                .STARTED
            ),
            attempt=1,
            error_code=None,
        ),
        reference_at=REFERENCE + timedelta(seconds=60),
    )

    assert (
        result.decision
        is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision.ELIGIBLE
    )

    assert result.backoff_seconds == 60

    assert result.next_eligible_at == (
        REFERENCE + timedelta(seconds=60)
    )


def test_started_recovery_can_be_disabled_by_policy():
    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService(
        policy=policy(
            retry_interrupted_started=False
        )
    )

    result = service.evaluate(
        record=record(
            status=(
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus
                .STARTED
            ),
            error_code=None,
        ),
        reference_at=REFERENCE + timedelta(seconds=60),
    )

    assert (
        result.decision
        is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision.NOT_ELIGIBLE
    )


def test_succeeded_delivery_is_never_retryable():
    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService(
        policy=policy()
    )

    result = service.evaluate(
        record=record(
            status=(
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus
                .SUCCEEDED
            ),
            error_code=None,
        ),
        reference_at=REFERENCE,
    )

    assert (
        result.decision
        is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision.NOT_ELIGIBLE
    )


def test_missing_delivery_timestamp_is_not_retryable():
    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService(
        policy=policy()
    )

    result = service.evaluate(
        record=Record(
            notification_key=(
                "platform_intelligence_escalation:REC-1"
            ),
            recommendation_id="REC-1",
            risk_decision_id="DEC-1",
            review_item_id="REV-1",
            correlation_id="CORR-1",
            attempt=1,
            status=(
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus
                .FAILED
            ),
            error_code="PROVIDER_ERROR",
            created_at=None,
            updated_at=None,
        ),
        reference_at=REFERENCE,
    )

    assert (
        result.decision
        is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision.NOT_ELIGIBLE
    )

    assert "updated_at" in result.reason


def test_reference_time_must_be_timezone_aware():
    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService(
        policy=policy()
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="timezone-aware",
    ):
        service.evaluate(
            record=record(),
            reference_at=datetime(
                2026,
                9,
                22,
                10,
                0,
            ),
        )


def test_policy_rejects_invalid_backoff_configuration():
    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="max_backoff_seconds",
    ):
        policy(
            base_backoff_seconds=300,
            max_backoff_seconds=60,
        )


def test_policy_rejects_invalid_attempt_limit():
    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="max_attempts",
    ):
        policy(
            max_attempts=0
        )

FILE: tests\unit\operations_center\test_platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_candidate_discovery.py

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone

import pytest

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_candidate_discovery import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidateDiscoveryService,
)

T1 = datetime(2026, 9, 22, 10, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
T2 = datetime(2026, 9, 22, 10, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc)


@dataclass
class Record:
    notification_key: str
    recommendation_id: str
    risk_decision_id: str
    review_item_id: str
    correlation_id: str
    attempt: int
    status: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus
    provider_message_id: str | None = None
    error_code: str | None = None
    created_at: datetime | None = T1
    updated_at: datetime | None = T1


class Repository:
    def __init__(self, records):
        self.records = records
        self.limit = None

    async def list_retry_candidates(self, *, limit):
        self.limit = limit
        return self.records


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_discovery_returns_only_failed_and_started_candidates():
    repository = Repository(
        [
            Record(
                "key-succeeded",
                "rec-s",
                "dec-s",
                "rev-s",
                "corr-s",
                1,
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.SUCCEEDED,
                updated_at=T1,
            ),
            Record(
                "key-failed",
                "rec-f",
                "dec-f",
                "rev-f",
                "corr-f",
                1,
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.FAILED,
                error_code="PROVIDER_ERROR",
                updated_at=T2,
            ),
            Record(
                "key-started",
                "rec-t",
                "dec-t",
                "rev-t",
                "corr-t",
                1,
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.STARTED,
                updated_at=T1,
            ),
        ]
    )

    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidateDiscoveryService(
        repository=repository
    )

    result = await service.discover(limit=50)

    assert repository.limit == 50
    assert [item.notification_key for item in result.candidates] == [
        "key-started",
        "key-failed",
    ]


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_discovery_is_deterministically_ordered():
    repository = Repository(
        [
            Record(
                "key-b",
                "rec-b",
                "dec-b",
                "rev-b",
                "corr-b",
                1,
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.FAILED,
                updated_at=T1,
            ),
            Record(
                "key-a",
                "rec-a",
                "dec-a",
                "rev-a",
                "corr-a",
                1,
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.FAILED,
                updated_at=T1,
            ),
        ]
    )

    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidateDiscoveryService(
        repository=repository
    )

    result = await service.discover(limit=10)

    assert [item.notification_key for item in result.candidates] == [
        "key-a",
        "key-b",
    ]


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_discovery_preserves_retry_metadata():
    repository = Repository(
        [
            Record(
                "key-1",
                "rec-1",
                "dec-1",
                "rev-1",
                "corr-1",
                2,
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.FAILED,
                error_code="TIMEOUT",
                updated_at=T2,
            )
        ]
    )

    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidateDiscoveryService(
        repository=repository
    )

    result = await service.discover(limit=10)
    candidate = result.candidates[0]

    assert candidate.attempt == 2
    assert candidate.error_code == "TIMEOUT"
    assert candidate.updated_at == T2
    assert candidate.status == "FAILED"


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_discovery_rejects_invalid_limit():
    repository = Repository([])

    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidateDiscoveryService(
        repository=repository
    )

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="at least 1"):
        await service.discover(limit=0)

from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

import pytest

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_claims import (
    RetryCandidateClaimStatus,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_claim_service import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryClaimService,
)


class FakeClaimRepository:
    def __init__(self, result: bool) -> None:
        self.result = result
        self.calls = []

    async def claim_retry_candidate(self, **kwargs):
        self.calls.append(kwargs)
        return self.result


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_claim_returns_bounded_lease() -> None:
    repository = FakeClaimRepository(True)
    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryClaimService(
        repository,
        lease_seconds=60,
    )
    reference = datetime(2026, 9, 22, 12, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)

    claim = await service.claim(
        notification_key="pi-key",
        claimed_at=reference,
    )

    assert claim is not None
    assert claim.notification_key == "pi-key"
    assert claim.claimed_at == reference
    assert claim.lease_expires_at == reference + timedelta(seconds=60)
    assert claim.status is RetryCandidateClaimStatus.CLAIMED
    assert repository.calls[0]["notification_key"] == "pi-key"


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_claim_returns_none_when_candidate_is_already_claimed() -> None:
    repository = FakeClaimRepository(False)
    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryClaimService(repository)

    claim = await service.claim(
        notification_key="pi-key",
        claimed_at=datetime(2026, 9, 22, 12, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc),
    )

    assert claim is None


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_claim_rejects_naive_reference_time() -> None:
    repository = FakeClaimRepository(True)
    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryClaimService(repository)

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="timezone-aware"):
        await service.claim(
            notification_key="pi-key",
            claimed_at=datetime(2026, 9, 22, 12, 0),
        )

FILE: tests\unit\operations_center\test_platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_execution.py

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone

import pytest

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_dispatch import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchRequest,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchResult,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchStatus,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicy,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyStatus,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_execution import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionService,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionStatus,
)


DELIVERY_TIME = datetime(
    2026,
    9,
    22,
    10,
    0,
    tzinfo=timezone.utc,
)

REFERENCE = datetime(
    2026,
    9,
    22,
    10,
    1,
    tzinfo=timezone.utc,
)


@dataclass
class Record:
    notification_key: str
    recommendation_id: str
    risk_decision_id: str
    review_item_id: str
    correlation_id: str
    attempt: int
    status: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus
    provider_message_id: str | None = None
    error_code: str | None = None
    created_at: datetime | None = DELIVERY_TIME
    updated_at: datetime | None = DELIVERY_TIME


class Repository:
    def __init__(self, record):
        self.record = record
        self.retries = 0

    async def get_by_notification_key(self, key):
        if self.record is None:
            return None

        if self.record.notification_key == key:
            return self.record

        return None

    async def start_retry(self, *, notification_key):
        self.retries += 1

        self.record.attempt += 1
        self.record.status = (
            PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.STARTED
        )
        self.record.error_code = None
        self.record.updated_at = REFERENCE

        return self.record


class Dispatch:
    def __init__(self):
        self.calls = 0

    async def dispatch(self, *, request, email_request):
        self.calls += 1

        return PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchResult(
            status=PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchStatus.SENT,
            notification_key=request.notification_key,
            provider_message_id="provider-1",
            attempt=2,
        )


def policy():
    return PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService(
        policy=PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicy(
            policy_id="PI-RETRY",
            policy_version="1.0",
            status=(
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyStatus
                .APPROVED
            ),
            max_attempts=4,
            base_backoff_seconds=60,
            max_backoff_seconds=300,
            retryable_error_codes=frozenset(
                {"PROVIDER_ERROR"}
            ),
            retry_interrupted_started=True,
        )
    )


def request():
    return PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchRequest(
        recommendation_id="REC-1",
        risk_decision_id="DEC-1",
        review_item_id="REV-1",
        risk_level="HIGH",
        risk_score=0.82,
        quality_gate_status="PASSED",
        review_status="IN_REVIEW",
        decision_status="PENDING_HUMAN_REVIEW",
        notification_key=(
            "platform_intelligence_escalation:REC-1"
        ),
        correlation_id="CORR-1",
        route="RISK_OWNER",
        reasons=("High risk.",),
    )


def record(
    status,
    attempt=1,
    error_code="PROVIDER_ERROR",
):
    return Record(
        notification_key=(
            "platform_intelligence_escalation:REC-1"
        ),
        recommendation_id="REC-1",
        risk_decision_id="DEC-1",
        review_item_id="REV-1",
        correlation_id="CORR-1",
        attempt=attempt,
        status=status,
        error_code=error_code,
        created_at=DELIVERY_TIME,
        updated_at=DELIVERY_TIME,
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_failed_delivery_retries_only_when_due():
    repository = Repository(
        record(
            PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.FAILED
        )
    )

    dispatch = Dispatch()

    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionService(
        delivery_repository=repository,
        retry_policy=policy(),
        dispatch_service=dispatch,
    )

    early = await service.retry(
        request=request(),
        email_request=object(),
        reference_at=datetime(
            2026,
            9,
            22,
            10,
            0,
            30,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        ),
    )

    assert (
        early.status
        is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionStatus
        .NOT_DUE
    )

    assert repository.retries == 0
    assert dispatch.calls == 0

    due = await service.retry(
        request=request(),
        email_request=object(),
        reference_at=REFERENCE,
    )

    assert (
        due.status
        is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionStatus
        .RETRIED
    )

    assert repository.retries == 1
    assert dispatch.calls == 1


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_non_retryable_failure_does_not_dispatch():
    repository = Repository(
        record(
            PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.FAILED,
            error_code="INVALID_RECIPIENT",
        )
    )

    dispatch = Dispatch()

    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionService(
        delivery_repository=repository,
        retry_policy=policy(),
        dispatch_service=dispatch,
    )

    result = await service.retry(
        request=request(),
        email_request=object(),
        reference_at=REFERENCE,
    )

    assert (
        result.status
        is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionStatus
        .NOT_ELIGIBLE
    )

    assert repository.retries == 0
    assert dispatch.calls == 0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_started_recovery_does_not_increment_attempt():
    repository = Repository(
        record(
            PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.STARTED,
            error_code=None,
        )
    )

    dispatch = Dispatch()

    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionService(
        delivery_repository=repository,
        retry_policy=policy(),
        dispatch_service=dispatch,
    )

    result = await service.retry(
        request=request(),
        email_request=object(),
        reference_at=REFERENCE,
    )

    assert (
        result.status
        is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionStatus
        .RETRIED
    )

    assert repository.retries == 0
    assert repository.record.attempt == 1
    assert dispatch.calls == 1


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_max_attempts_prevents_retry():
    repository = Repository(
        record(
            PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.FAILED,
            attempt=4,
        )
    )

    dispatch = Dispatch()

    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionService(
        delivery_repository=repository,
        retry_policy=policy(),
        dispatch_service=dispatch,
    )

    result = await service.retry(
        request=request(),
        email_request=object(),
        reference_at=REFERENCE,
    )

    assert (
        result.status
        is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionStatus
        .NOT_ELIGIBLE
    )

    assert repository.retries == 0
    assert dispatch.calls == 0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_retry_requires_existing_durable_record():
    repository = Repository(None)
    dispatch = Dispatch()

    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionService(
        delivery_repository=repository,
        retry_policy=policy(),
        dispatch_service=dispatch,
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="existing durable",
    ):
        await service.retry(
            request=request(),
            email_request=object(),
            reference_at=REFERENCE,
        )

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone

import pytest

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_dispatch import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchRequest,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_candidates import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidate,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_execution import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionResult,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionStatus,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_orchestration import (
    PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunStatus,
    PlatformIntelligenceRetryState,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_orchestrator import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryOrchestrator,
)


REFERENCE = datetime(2026, 9, 22, 12, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)

NOTIFICATION_KEY = (
    "platform_intelligence_escalation:recommendation-1"
)


def dispatch_request() -> PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchRequest:
    return PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchRequest(
        recommendation_id="recommendation-1",
        risk_decision_id="decision-1",
        review_item_id="review-1",
        risk_level="HIGH",
        risk_score=0.82,
        quality_gate_status="PASSED",
        review_status="IN_REVIEW",
        decision_status="PENDING_HUMAN_REVIEW",
        notification_key=NOTIFICATION_KEY,
        correlation_id="correlation-1",
        route="RISK_OWNER",
        reasons=("High risk level.",),
    )


def candidate() -> PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidate:
    return PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidate(
        notification_key=NOTIFICATION_KEY,
        recommendation_id="recommendation-1",
        risk_decision_id="decision-1",
        review_item_id="review-1",
        correlation_id="correlation-1",
        attempt=1,
        status="FAILED",
        updated_at=REFERENCE,
        error_code="PROVIDER_ERROR",
    )


class FakePolicy:
    def __init__(self, eligible: bool) -> None:
        self.eligible = eligible
        self.calls = 0

    async def evaluate(self, **kwargs):
        self.calls += 1
        return self.eligible


class FakeClaimService:
    def __init__(self, claim) -> None:
        self.claim_result = claim
        self.calls = 0

    async def claim(self, **kwargs):
        self.calls += 1
        return self.claim_result


class FakeStateReader:
    def __init__(self, state) -> None:
        self.state = state
        self.calls = 0

    async def get_retry_state(self, **kwargs):
        self.calls += 1
        return self.state


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class FakeClaim:
    token: str = "claim-token"


class FakeRetryExecution:
    def __init__(
        self,
        result: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionResult,
    ) -> None:
        self.result = result
        self.calls = 0
        self.kwargs = None

    async def retry(self, **kwargs):
        self.calls += 1
        self.kwargs = kwargs
        return self.result


def valid_state() -> PlatformIntelligenceRetryState:
    return PlatformIntelligenceRetryState(
        notification_key=NOTIFICATION_KEY,
        recommendation_id="recommendation-1",
        recommendation_status="RECOMMENDED",
        review_status="IN_REVIEW",
        decision_status="PENDING_HUMAN_REVIEW",
        quality_gate_status="PASSED",
        dispatch_request=dispatch_request(),
        email_request=object(),
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_not_policy_eligible_does_not_claim_or_execute() -> None:
    policy = FakePolicy(False)
    claims = FakeClaimService(FakeClaim())
    state = FakeStateReader(valid_state())

    execution = FakeRetryExecution(
        PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionResult(
            status=PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionStatus.RETRIED,
            notification_key=NOTIFICATION_KEY,
            attempt=1,
            reason="SUCCEEDED",
        )
    )

    orchestrator = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryOrchestrator(
        state_reader=state,
        policy_evaluator=policy,
        claim_service=claims,
        retry_execution=execution,
    )

    result = await orchestrator.run_candidate(
        candidate=candidate(),
        reference_at=REFERENCE,
    )

    assert result.status is PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunStatus.SKIPPED
    assert result.reason == "RETRY_POLICY_NOT_ELIGIBLE"
    assert claims.calls == 0
    assert state.calls == 0
    assert execution.calls == 0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_already_claimed_does_not_revalidate_or_execute() -> None:
    policy = FakePolicy(True)
    claims = FakeClaimService(None)
    state = FakeStateReader(valid_state())

    execution = FakeRetryExecution(
        PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionResult(
            status=PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionStatus.RETRIED,
            notification_key=NOTIFICATION_KEY,
            attempt=1,
            reason="SUCCEEDED",
        )
    )

    orchestrator = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryOrchestrator(
        state_reader=state,
        policy_evaluator=policy,
        claim_service=claims,
        retry_execution=execution,
    )

    result = await orchestrator.run_candidate(
        candidate=candidate(),
        reference_at=REFERENCE,
    )

    assert result.status is PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunStatus.SKIPPED
    assert result.reason == "RETRY_CANDIDATE_ALREADY_CLAIMED"
    assert state.calls == 0
    assert execution.calls == 0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_invalid_authoritative_state_blocks_execution() -> None:
    policy = FakePolicy(True)
    claims = FakeClaimService(FakeClaim())

    state = FakeStateReader(
        PlatformIntelligenceRetryState(
            notification_key=NOTIFICATION_KEY,
            recommendation_id="recommendation-1",
            recommendation_status="NOT_RECOMMENDED",
            review_status="IN_REVIEW",
            decision_status="PENDING_HUMAN_REVIEW",
            quality_gate_status="PASSED",
            dispatch_request=dispatch_request(),
            email_request=object(),
        )
    )

    execution = FakeRetryExecution(
        PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionResult(
            status=PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionStatus.RETRIED,
            notification_key=NOTIFICATION_KEY,
            attempt=1,
            reason="SUCCEEDED",
        )
    )

    orchestrator = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryOrchestrator(
        state_reader=state,
        policy_evaluator=policy,
        claim_service=claims,
        retry_execution=execution,
    )

    result = await orchestrator.run_candidate(
        candidate=candidate(),
        reference_at=REFERENCE,
    )

    assert result.status is PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunStatus.SKIPPED
    assert result.reason == "RECOMMENDATION_NO_LONGER_RECOMMENDED"
    assert execution.calls == 0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_valid_state_executes_after_claim() -> None:
    policy = FakePolicy(True)
    claims = FakeClaimService(FakeClaim())
    state = FakeStateReader(valid_state())

    execution = FakeRetryExecution(
        PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionResult(
            status=PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionStatus.RETRIED,
            notification_key=NOTIFICATION_KEY,
            attempt=2,
            reason="RETRY_DISPATCHED",
            dispatch_result=type(
                "DispatchResult",
                (),
                {"provider_message_id": "provider-message-2"},
            )(),
        )
    )

    orchestrator = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryOrchestrator(
        state_reader=state,
        policy_evaluator=policy,
        claim_service=claims,
        retry_execution=execution,
    )

    result = await orchestrator.run_candidate(
        candidate=candidate(),
        reference_at=REFERENCE,
    )

    assert result.status is PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunStatus.SUCCEEDED
    assert result.reason == "RETRY_DISPATCHED"
    assert result.attempt == 2
    assert result.provider_message_id == "provider-message-2"

    assert claims.calls == 1
    assert state.calls == 1
    assert execution.calls == 1

    assert execution.kwargs["request"] == dispatch_request()
    assert execution.kwargs["email_request"] is state.state.email_request
    assert execution.kwargs["reference_at"] == REFERENCE


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_naive_reference_time_is_rejected() -> None:
    orchestrator = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryOrchestrator(
        state_reader=FakeStateReader(valid_state()),
        policy_evaluator=FakePolicy(True),
        claim_service=FakeClaimService(FakeClaim()),
        retry_execution=FakeRetryExecution(
            PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionResult(
                status=PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionStatus.RETRIED,
                notification_key=NOTIFICATION_KEY,
                attempt=1,
                reason="SUCCEEDED",
            )
        ),
    )

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="timezone-aware"):
        await orchestrator.run_candidate(
            candidate=candidate(),
            reference_at=datetime(2026, 9, 22, 12, 0),
        )

FILE: tests\academy\test_academy_scheduler_invocation.py

from __future__ import annotations

from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, MagicMock

import pytest

from cybersecurity.scheduler.cybersecurity_apscheduler_adapter import (
    CybersecurityAPSchedulerAdapter,
)


pytestmark = pytest.mark.integration


def test_academy_scheduler_job_points_to_workflow_run():
    """
    The Academy scheduler job must point to the actual async
    AcademyOneWeekWorkflow.run() method.
    """

    scheduler = MagicMock(
        name="shared_scheduler",
    )

    workflow_service = MagicMock(
        name="cybersecurity_workflow_service",
    )

    academy_workflow = MagicMock(
        name="academy_one_week_workflow",
    )

    academy_workflow.run = AsyncMock(
        name="academy_workflow_run",
        return_value={
            "candidates": 0,
            "attempted": 0,
            "succeeded": 0,
            "failed": 0,
            "skipped": 0,
        },
    )

    adapter = CybersecurityAPSchedulerAdapter(
        scheduler=scheduler,
        workflow_service=workflow_service,
        academy_one_week_workflow=academy_workflow,
    )

    adapter.register_jobs()

    academy_calls = [
        call
        for call in scheduler.add_job.call_args_list
        if call.kwargs.get("id") == "academy_one_week_milestone"
    ]

    assert len(academy_calls) == 1

    academy_call = academy_calls[0]

    assert academy_call.args[0] is academy_workflow.run
    assert academy_call.kwargs["trigger"] == "cron"
    assert academy_call.kwargs["hour"] == 8
    assert academy_call.kwargs["minute"] == 15


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_registered_academy_job_can_invoke_workflow_without_external_delivery():
    """
    Invoke the registered callback directly.

    This simulates APScheduler executing the job while avoiding
    production scheduler startup and all external delivery.
    """

    scheduler = MagicMock(
        name="shared_scheduler",
    )

    workflow_service = MagicMock(
        name="cybersecurity_workflow_service",
    )

    academy_workflow = MagicMock(
        name="academy_one_week_workflow",
    )

    academy_workflow.run = AsyncMock(
        name="academy_workflow_run",
        return_value={
            "candidates": 0,
            "attempted": 0,
            "succeeded": 0,
            "failed": 0,
            "skipped": 0,
        },
    )

    adapter = CybersecurityAPSchedulerAdapter(
        scheduler=scheduler,
        workflow_service=workflow_service,
        academy_one_week_workflow=academy_workflow,
    )

    adapter.register_jobs()

    academy_call = next(
        call
        for call in scheduler.add_job.call_args_list
        if call.kwargs.get("id") == "academy_one_week_milestone"
    )

    registered_callback = academy_call.args[0]

    result = await registered_callback()

    assert result == {
        "candidates": 0,
        "attempted": 0,
        "succeeded": 0,
        "failed": 0,
        "skipped": 0,
    }

    academy_workflow.run.assert_awaited_once_with()


def test_academy_registration_does_not_start_scheduler():
    """
    Registering the Academy callback must not start APScheduler.
    """

    scheduler = MagicMock(
        name="shared_scheduler",
    )

    workflow_service = MagicMock(
        name="cybersecurity_workflow_service",
    )

    academy_workflow = MagicMock(
        name="academy_one_week_workflow",
    )

    adapter = CybersecurityAPSchedulerAdapter(
        scheduler=scheduler,
        workflow_service=workflow_service,
        academy_one_week_workflow=academy_workflow,
    )

    adapter.register_jobs()

    scheduler.start.assert_not_called()
    scheduler.shutdown.assert_not_called()


def test_academy_registration_keeps_existing_scheduler_jobs():
    """
    Academy registration must preserve the existing cybersecurity jobs.
    """

    scheduler = MagicMock(
        name="shared_scheduler",
    )

    workflow_service = MagicMock(
        name="cybersecurity_workflow_service",
    )

    academy_workflow = MagicMock(
        name="academy_one_week_workflow",
    )

    adapter = CybersecurityAPSchedulerAdapter(
        scheduler=scheduler,
        workflow_service=workflow_service,
        academy_one_week_workflow=academy_workflow,
    )

    adapter.register_jobs()

    registered_ids = {
        call.kwargs["id"]
        for call in scheduler.add_job.call_args_list
    }

    assert {
        "cybersecurity_daily_report",
        "cybersecurity_weekly_report",
        "academy_one_week_milestone",
    }.issubset(registered_ids)

from __future__ import annotations

from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, MagicMock

import pytest

from cybersecurity.scheduler.cybersecurity_scheduler_runtime import (
    CybersecuritySchedulerRuntime,
)


pytestmark = pytest.mark.integration


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_shared_scheduler_lifecycle_starts_and_stops_cleanly():
    """
    The shared scheduler runtime must start and stop cleanly.

    Academy is already injected into the existing scheduler boundary.
    """

    adapter = MagicMock(
        name="scheduler_adapter",
    )

    adapter.start = MagicMock(
        name="start",
    )

    adapter.shutdown = MagicMock(
        name="shutdown",
    )

    runtime = CybersecuritySchedulerRuntime(
        scheduler_adapter=adapter,
    )

    await runtime.start()

    adapter.start.assert_called_once()

    await runtime.stop()

    adapter.shutdown.assert_called_once()


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_scheduler_lifecycle_does_not_execute_academy_workflow():
    """
    Starting/stopping the scheduler lifecycle must not directly execute
    the Academy workflow.
    """

    scheduler = MagicMock(
        name="shared_scheduler",
    )

    scheduler.running = False

    academy_workflow = MagicMock(
        name="academy_one_week_workflow",
    )

    adapter = MagicMock(
        name="scheduler_adapter",
    )

    adapter.scheduler = scheduler
    adapter.academy_one_week_workflow = academy_workflow

    adapter.start = MagicMock(
        name="start",
    )

    adapter.shutdown = MagicMock(
        name="shutdown",
    )

    runtime = CybersecuritySchedulerRuntime(
        scheduler_adapter=adapter,
    )

    await runtime.start()
    await runtime.stop()

    academy_workflow.run.assert_not_called()


def test_scheduler_runtime_uses_existing_adapter():
    """
    Runtime must use the supplied scheduler adapter rather than creating
    scheduler infrastructure itself.
    """

    adapter = MagicMock(
        name="existing_scheduler_adapter",
    )

    runtime = CybersecuritySchedulerRuntime(
        scheduler_adapter=adapter,
    )

    assert runtime.scheduler_adapter is adapter


============================================================
FILE: tests\academy\test_academy_scheduler_registration.py
============================================================
from __future__ import annotations

from unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch

import pytest

from cybersecurity.scheduler.cybersecurity_scheduler_factory import (
    create_cybersecurity_scheduler_runtime,
)


pytestmark = pytest.mark.integration


def _create_controlled_scheduler():
    scheduler = MagicMock(
        name="shared_scheduler",
    )

    scheduler.running = False

    return scheduler


def test_academy_workflow_is_injected_into_existing_scheduler():
    """
    Academy workflow must be injected into the existing scheduler
    runtime boundary.
    """

    scheduler = _create_controlled_scheduler()

    academy_workflow = MagicMock(
        name="academy_one_week_workflow",
    )

    with patch(
        "cybersecurity.scheduler.cybersecurity_scheduler_factory.AsyncIOScheduler",
        return_value=scheduler,
    ):

        runtime = create_cybersecurity_scheduler_runtime(
            workflow_service=MagicMock(
                name="cybersecurity_workflow",
            ),
            academy_one_week_workflow=academy_workflow,
        )

    adapter = runtime.scheduler_adapter

    assert adapter.scheduler is scheduler

    assert (
        adapter.academy_one_week_workflow
        is academy_workflow
    )


def test_academy_job_registration_does_not_start_scheduler():
    """
    Academy registration must add exactly one Academy job and must not
    start or execute the scheduler.
    """

    scheduler = _create_controlled_scheduler()

    academy_workflow = MagicMock(
        name="academy_one_week_workflow",
    )

    with patch(
        "cybersecurity.scheduler.cybersecurity_scheduler_factory.AsyncIOScheduler",
        return_value=scheduler,
    ):

        runtime = create_cybersecurity_scheduler_runtime(
            workflow_service=MagicMock(
                name="cybersecurity_workflow",
            ),
            academy_one_week_workflow=academy_workflow,
        )

    adapter = runtime.scheduler_adapter

    adapter.register_jobs()

    academy_calls = [
        call
        for call in scheduler.add_job.call_args_list
        if call.kwargs.get("id")
        == "academy_one_week_milestone"
    ]

    assert len(academy_calls) == 1

    academy_call = academy_calls[0]

    assert (
        academy_call.args[0]
        is academy_workflow.run
    )

    assert (
        academy_call.kwargs["trigger"]
        == "cron"
    )

    assert (
        academy_call.kwargs["hour"]
        == adapter.academy_one_week_hour
    )

    assert (
        academy_call.kwargs["minute"]
        == adapter.academy_one_week_minute
    )

    assert (
        academy_call.kwargs["id"]
        == "academy_one_week_milestone"
    )

    assert (
        academy_call.kwargs["replace_existing"]
        is True
    )

    assert scheduler.start.called is False

    assert scheduler.running is False

    assert academy_workflow.run.called is False


def test_academy_and_cybersecurity_jobs_share_one_scheduler():
    """
    Academy and existing cybersecurity jobs must be registered on
    the same scheduler instance.
    """

    scheduler = _create_controlled_scheduler()

    cybersecurity_workflow = MagicMock(
        name="cybersecurity_workflow",
    )

    academy_workflow = MagicMock(
        name="academy_one_week_workflow",
    )

    with patch(
        "cybersecurity.scheduler.cybersecurity_scheduler_factory.AsyncIOScheduler",
        return_value=scheduler,
    ):

        runtime = create_cybersecurity_scheduler_runtime(
            workflow_service=cybersecurity_workflow,
            academy_one_week_workflow=academy_workflow,
        )

    adapter = runtime.scheduler_adapter

    assert adapter.scheduler is scheduler

    adapter.register_jobs()

    registered_ids = {
        call.kwargs.get("id")
        for call in scheduler.add_job.call_args_list
    }

    assert (
        "cybersecurity_daily_report"
        in registered_ids
    )

    assert (
        "cybersecurity_weekly_report"
        in registered_ids
    )

    assert (
        "academy_one_week_milestone"
        in registered_ids
    )

    assert scheduler.start.called is False
    assert scheduler.running is False

    assert cybersecurity_workflow.create_daily_report.called is False
    assert cybersecurity_workflow.create_weekly_report.called is False
    assert academy_workflow.run.called is False



============================================================
FILE: tests\academy\test_academy_scheduler_smoke.py
============================================================
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
from unittest.mock import MagicMock

import pytest

from cybersecurity.scheduler.cybersecurity_scheduler_factory import (
    create_cybersecurity_scheduler_runtime,
)


pytestmark = pytest.mark.integration


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_shared_scheduler_start_registers_academy_and_stops_cleanly():
    """
    Controlled real APScheduler lifecycle smoke test.

    Verifies:
    - one shared scheduler;
    - Academy job registration;
    - existing cybersecurity jobs registration;
    - scheduler starts inside a running asyncio event loop;
    - scheduler becomes running;
    - Academy workflow is not executed;
    - shutdown is invoked cleanly.

    No real email or Telegram delivery is performed.
    """

    academy_workflow = MagicMock(
        name="academy_one_week_workflow",
    )

    runtime = create_cybersecurity_scheduler_runtime(
        workflow_service=MagicMock(
            name="cybersecurity_workflow",
        ),
        academy_one_week_workflow=academy_workflow,
    )

    adapter = runtime.scheduler_adapter

    try:
        adapter.register_jobs()

        jobs = adapter.scheduler.get_jobs()

        job_ids = {
            job.id
            for job in jobs
        }

        assert (
            "academy_one_week_milestone"
            in job_ids
        )

        assert (
            "cybersecurity_daily_report"
            in job_ids
        )

        assert (
            "cybersecurity_weekly_report"
            in job_ids
        )

        assert adapter.scheduler.running is False

        adapter.start()

        assert adapter.scheduler.running is True

        # Starting the scheduler must not execute Academy immediately.
        assert academy_workflow.run.call_count == 0

    finally:
        adapter.shutdown()

        # Give the asyncio scheduler event loop one opportunity to
        # process its shutdown state.
        await asyncio.sleep(0)

    # The important lifecycle contract is that shutdown was invoked
    # and no Academy execution occurred.
    assert academy_workflow.run.call_count == 0
