FILE: tests\academy\test_academy_real_lifespan_scheduler_smoke.py
============================================================
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
from unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch

import pytest

from app.infrastructure.telegram.telegram_announcement_client import (
    TelegramAnnouncementClient,
)


pytestmark = pytest.mark.integration


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_academy_real_shared_scheduler_lifespan_smoke():
    """
    Controlled end-to-end scheduler lifecycle smoke test.

    Uses:
    - real FastAPI lifespan;
    - real CybersecuritySchedulerRuntime;
    - real CybersecurityAPSchedulerAdapter;
    - real APScheduler.

    Academy execution and external delivery remain mocked.

    No real email or Telegram delivery occurs.
    """

    fake_engine = MagicMock(
        name="auth_engine",
    )

    fake_session_factory = MagicMock(
        name="auth_session_factory",
    )

    telegram_client = MagicMock(
        spec=TelegramAnnouncementClient,
        name="announcement_telegram_client",
    )

    announcement_publisher = MagicMock(
        name="announcement_publisher",
    )

    academy_workflow = MagicMock(
        name="academy_one_week_workflow",
    )

    academy_execution_count = 0

    async def academy_run():
        nonlocal academy_execution_count

        academy_execution_count += 1

        return {
            "candidates": 0,
            "attempted": 0,
            "succeeded": 0,
            "failed": 0,
            "skipped": 0,
        }

    academy_workflow.run = academy_run

    with (
        patch.dict(
            "os.environ",
            {
                "EASYCHATBOT_ACADEMY_SCHEDULER_ENABLED": "true",
                "EASYCHATBOT_ACADEMY_FIRST_RUN_ENABLED": "true",
            },
            clear=False,
        ),
        patch(
            "app.main.create_async_database_engine",
            return_value=fake_engine,
        ),
        patch(
            "app.main.create_async_session_factory",
            return_value=fake_session_factory,
        ),
        patch(
            "app.main.PostgreSQLAuthenticationRepository",
            return_value=MagicMock(
                name="auth_repository",
            ),
        ),
        patch(
            "app.main._create_announcement_components",
            return_value=(
                telegram_client,
                announcement_publisher,
            ),
        ),
        patch(
            "app.main.create_academy_one_week_workflow",
            return_value=academy_workflow,
        ),
    ):
        from app.main import create_application

        application = create_application()

        runtime = application.state.scheduler_runtime
        adapter = runtime.scheduler_adapter
        scheduler = adapter.scheduler

        #
        # Application construction must not start scheduling.
        #

        assert scheduler.running is False

        async with application.router.lifespan_context(
            application,
        ):
            #
            # FastAPI lifespan starts the existing scheduler.
            #

            assert scheduler.running is True

            jobs = scheduler.get_jobs()

            job_ids = {
                job.id
                for job in jobs
            }

            #
            # Existing cybersecurity jobs remain registered.
            #

            assert (
                "cybersecurity_daily_report"
                in job_ids
            )

            assert (
                "cybersecurity_weekly_report"
                in job_ids
            )

            #
            # Academy is registered on the same scheduler.
            #

            assert (
                "academy_one_week_milestone"
                in job_ids
            )

            assert (
                adapter.academy_one_week_workflow
                is academy_workflow
            )

            #
            # Starting the scheduler must not execute the Academy
            # cron job immediately.
            #

            await asyncio.sleep(0.05)

            assert academy_execution_count == 0

            #
            # No Telegram delivery may occur.
            #
            # close() is intentionally excluded because it is normal
            # FastAPI lifespan cleanup and is not a delivery operation.
            #

            telegram_delivery_calls = [
                call
                for call in telegram_client.method_calls
                if call[0] not in {"close"}
            ]

            assert telegram_delivery_calls == []

        #
        # FastAPI lifespan has exited and requested scheduler shutdown.
        #

        for _ in range(20):
            if scheduler.running is False:
                break

            await asyncio.sleep(0.01)

        assert scheduler.running is False

    #
    # Academy must never have executed during this smoke test.
    #

    assert academy_execution_count == 0

    #
    # Telegram close is expected lifecycle cleanup.
    #

    telegram_client.close.assert_called_once()

    #
    # No Telegram delivery operation occurred.
    #

    telegram_delivery_calls = [
        call
        for call in telegram_client.method_calls
        if call[0] not in {"close"}
    ]

    assert telegram_delivery_calls == []

FILE: tests\academy\test_academy_real_postgresql_retry.py

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from uuid import uuid4

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import text

from app.application.academy.academy_one_week_workflow import (
    AcademyOneWeekWorkflow,
)
from app.infrastructure.academy.postgresql_academy_notification_repository import (
    PostgreSQLAcademyDeliveryRepository,
    PostgreSQLAcademyUserQuery,
)
from app.infrastructure.persistence.database import (
    create_async_database_engine,
    create_async_session_factory,
)


pytestmark = pytest.mark.integration


class ControlledNotifications:
    """Fail once, then succeed."""

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.calls = 0

    @staticmethod
    def is_one_week_eligible(
        *,
        registered_at: datetime,
        now: datetime,
    ) -> bool:
        age = now - registered_at

        return (
            timedelta(days=7)
            <= age
            < timedelta(days=8)
        )

    async def send_one_week(
        self,
        *,
        email: str,
        username: str,
        telegram_chat_id: str | None,
    ) -> dict:
        self.calls += 1

        if self.calls == 1:
            raise RuntimeError(
                "controlled integration failure"
            )

        return {
            "notification_type": "1_WEEK_MILESTONE",
            "email": {
                "status": "mocked",
                "message_id": "retry-integration-message-id",
            },
            "telegram": None,
        }


async def _create_test_user(
    session_factory,
    *,
    user_id,
    email: str,
    username: str,
    created_at: datetime,
) -> None:
    async with session_factory() as session:
        await session.execute(
            text(
                """
                INSERT INTO users (
                    id,
                    email,
                    username,
                    status,
                    trial_status,
                    created_at
                )
                VALUES (
                    :id,
                    :email,
                    :username,
                    'active',
                    'active',
                    :created_at
                )
                """
            ),
            {
                "id": user_id,
                "email": email,
                "username": username,
                "created_at": created_at,
            },
        )

        await session.commit()


async def _delete_test_data(
    session_factory,
    *,
    user_id,
) -> None:
    async with session_factory() as session:
        await session.execute(
            text(
                """
                DELETE FROM academy_notification_deliveries
                WHERE user_id = :user_id
                """
            ),
            {"user_id": user_id},
        )

        await session.execute(
            text(
                """
                DELETE FROM users
                WHERE id = :user_id
                """
            ),
            {"user_id": user_id},
        )

        await session.commit()


async def _get_delivery(
    session_factory,
    *,
    user_id,
):
    async with session_factory() as session:
        result = await session.execute(
            text(
                """
                SELECT
                    status,
                    attempt_count,
                    provider_message_id,
                    last_error,
                    sent_at
                FROM academy_notification_deliveries
                WHERE user_id = :user_id
                  AND notification_type = :notification_type
                """
            ),
            {
                "user_id": user_id,
                "notification_type": (
                    AcademyOneWeekWorkflow.NOTIFICATION_TYPE
                ),
            },
        )

        return result.first()


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_real_postgresql_failure_is_persisted_then_retry_succeeds():
    """
    Real PostgreSQL retry lifecycle.

    First run:
        claim -> notification failure -> FAILED

    Second run:
        retry -> notification success -> SUCCEEDED
    """

    engine = create_async_database_engine()
    session_factory = create_async_session_factory(engine)

    user_id = uuid4()

    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

    created_at = now - timedelta(
        days=7,
        hours=12,
    )

    email = (
        f"academy-retry-{user_id}"
        "@example.invalid"
    )

    username = (
        f"academy_retry_{user_id.hex[:12]}"
    )

    notifications = ControlledNotifications()

    try:
        await _create_test_user(
            session_factory,
            user_id=user_id,
            email=email,
            username=username,
            created_at=created_at,
        )

        workflow = AcademyOneWeekWorkflow(
            users=PostgreSQLAcademyUserQuery(
                session_factory,
            ),
            deliveries=PostgreSQLAcademyDeliveryRepository(
                session_factory,
            ),
            notifications=notifications,
            clock=lambda: now,
        )

        first_result = await workflow.run()

        assert first_result["candidates"] == 1
        assert first_result["attempted"] == 1
        assert first_result["succeeded"] == 0
        assert first_result["failed"] == 1
        assert first_result["skipped"] == 0

        delivery_after_failure = await _get_delivery(
            session_factory,
            user_id=user_id,
        )

        assert delivery_after_failure is not None
        assert delivery_after_failure.status == "FAILED"
        assert delivery_after_failure.attempt_count == 1
        assert (
            delivery_after_failure.provider_message_id
            is None
        )
        assert (
            delivery_after_failure.last_error
            == "controlled integration failure"
        )
        assert delivery_after_failure.sent_at is None

        second_result = await workflow.run()

        assert second_result["candidates"] == 1
        assert second_result["attempted"] == 1
        assert second_result["succeeded"] == 1
        assert second_result["failed"] == 0
        assert second_result["skipped"] == 0

        assert notifications.calls == 2

        delivery_after_retry = await _get_delivery(
            session_factory,
            user_id=user_id,
        )

        assert delivery_after_retry is not None
        assert delivery_after_retry.status == "SUCCEEDED"
        assert delivery_after_retry.attempt_count == 2
        assert (
            delivery_after_retry.provider_message_id
            == "retry-integration-message-id"
        )
        assert delivery_after_retry.last_error is None
        assert delivery_after_retry.sent_at is not None

    finally:
        await _delete_test_data(
            session_factory,
            user_id=user_id,
        )

        await engine.dispose()
